Chapter 2751: The Woman In The Corner

It was the sixth day of the ultimate test.

“It suddenly occurred to me that something is wrong with this test.”

As usual, Chu Zhiyun was chewing a lollipop in a fancy dress and watching soap operas from the Imperium with Tang Ka when she made the unexpected remark.

“W-What?”

Tang Ka was almost scared out of his wits. He looked around subconsciously, fearing that someone would show up and arrest them. “What’s wrong? What have you found?”

“I don’t know. I just don’t feel very well. We, the Children of Holy Light, have the best instincts. Have you felt anything wrong?”

Chu Zhiyun was sitting on an indoor swing. She said, “Many students who entered the town with us were gone in the past two days. This town is now awfully quiet.”

“It’s because they’re eliminated, right?”

Tang Ka thought for a moment and said, “The elimination rate in this ultimate test is very high in the first place!”

“That’s exactly the problem.”

Chu Zhiyun said, deep in thought, “If only one in a hundred people can become a Purger, how can we have so many purge troops?”

“Well…”

Tang Ka blinked his eyes. “That does seem to be a problem.”

“Of course it is. Just think about it. A thousand students participated in this ultimate test. Now that more than half of them have been eliminated, only one or two hundred of them can become Purgers. However, the purge troop nearby alone has more than three thousand soldiers.”

Chu Zhiyun counted her fingers and calculated for Tang Ka. “Also, this is just a tiny proportion of the purge troops. It’s impossible for all the Purgers to come to watch the ‘noobs’ like us, right? Most of the Purgers are still busy purging this planet. How many of them must be out there?

“If the purge troops are exclusively made of the Children of Holy Light, they wouldn’t be enough at all!”

Chu Zhiyun was better and better at impersonating an Imperium native. She could even say things like “noob” naturally now.

“Seems so…”

Recalling what Li Yao told him before, Tang Ka smiled both sadly and awkwardly. “There are probably other Children of Holy Light that we don’t know.”

“Oh? Really?”

Tapping her nose, Chu Zhiyun mumbled, “But everything just feels weird to me. I don’t like our current life and the world we grew up in.

“The world we grew up in was just a tiny starship, and all the ‘facts’ that we received were images and pictures. We accepted orders from mentors and directors through our portable crystal processors, and the mentors and directors accepted orders from the captain of the starship, and the starship then accepted orders from the Ultimate Benevolence Masters on another starship.

“But nobody has ever explained the meaning of those orders to us. Nobody told us how we can fulfill our destiny of awakening the gods. We’re only carrying out the uncanny orders from uncanny places in our ignorance. Is that really piety to the gods?

“Let’s just talk about the recent twenty years.

“Ever since we were born, a voice that claimed to be an Ultimate Benevolence Master has kept telling us the will of the gods, that we should unconditionally worship and obey the gods, and that we would conquer everything in the universe as the sharpest blade under the favor of the gods!

“But is it the truth? Both of us know that it is not. Before we were born, the Imperium of True Human Beings had taken almost a hundred Sectors from us in only twenty years. A hundred Sectors!

“If we’re really favored by the gods, why would we have suffered such a grave setback?

“The people above us, on the other hand, destroyed all the facilities on hundreds of planets just in order to stop the troops of the Imperium of True Human Beings from pressing forward!

“Isn’t it more outrageous than the people of the Imperium?

“The prosperous lands that took generations of people hundreds of years to work on were our best gifts for the gods. Those lands belonged to the gods. Were we really qualified to burn them to the ground?

“Ever since we were born, the Covenant Alliance’s fleets have been retreating, retreating, and retreating. We moved from one starship to another, but our living space got smaller and smaller and we had fewer and fewer resources, until we finally made a counterattack and reoccupied the Thick Soil Sector.

“However, you’ve seen the tragedies on this planet yourself. 99% of his planet is now contaminated, not by the Imperium but by ourselves. What is this for exactly?”

“You’re thinking too much.”

Naturally, it was not a question that Tang Ka could answer. He could only say, “The Ultimate Benevolence Masters decide the strategies in the war. It’s not our place to intervene.”

“I don’t believe it. I find it more and more impossible to believe the Ultimate Benevolence Masters, whom we have only heard but never seen.”

While speaking disobediently, Chu Zhiyun suddenly leaped off from the swing. “I’m starting to think that you may be right. The Ultimate Benevolence Masters might not want me to point out their mistakes. They may be infuriated.”

“Well…”

Tang Ka thought for a moment and asked, “What do you plan to do?”

“I don’t know.”

The seemingly mature girl was at a loss for the first time. She mumbled, “If the gods’ mission for me is to point out the Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ mistakes, I have to fulfill it whatever it takes. I can’t be purged in the holy temple in any case.”

She paced back and forth anxiously.

Tang Ka was even more desperate than she was.

Right when the young man and woman were in a trance, something unexpected happened.

In a huge noise, a row of shelves suddenly fell, and the items on the shelves were rolling on the ground.

The noise was as shocking as thunder.

Naturally, the shelves couldn’t have fallen unless someone accidentally touched them.

However, Tang Ka had locked the only gate, and they should be alone in this supermarket.

“Who’s there?”

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun looked at each other. Picking up their daggers and their storm bolters, they rushed to the shelves.

Both of them were fully aware of the consequences if someone else saw them in their current appearances, which were definitely not unnecessary for the recognition of dangerous items.

The person was running fast toward a corner in the supermarket while more shelves were falling.

It was not a shopping area but a cold room where the goods were stored.

Because the power for the supermarket had been cut off, the ice in the cold room had melted, and the frozen food inside was rotting and reeking of stink. Neither Tang Ka nor Chu Zhiyun was willing to stay here for long, so they hadn’t completely searched the cold room yet.

It was not until this moment that they realized someone was hiding in the cold room!

“Get out!”

Tang Ka roared, before he aimed his storm bolter at the gate of the cold room and pulled the trigger.

Boom!

Although he had set the storm gun to minimal power, the gate was still seriously deformed, and it was impossible to close it anymore.

The person tried to close the gate, but no matter how she exerted her strength, the door simply squeaked and wouldn’t budge, while cans and bottles of water were dropping from her clothes.

“Don’t move, or we’ll kill you!”

Tang Ka tried to look ruthless.

“Don’t… Don’t shoot! There’s a baby here! A baby!”

The woman shrieked in desperation and opened the gate of the cold room helplessly.

Both Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun aimed their flashlights at the stranger, only to see that she was a filthy middle-aged woman, who was sweating so hard as if she had just been picked up from a swamp.

“Wa! Wa! Wa! Wa!”

From the cold room behind her, the cries of a baby were coming!

Both Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun were greatly shocked, not knowing what to do at all.

“Don’t kill us. Please. We’re not soldiers. No soldiers are here. Only me and my daughter. Please let us go. Or you can capture me and let go of my daughter!”

The panicked and hideous woman suddenly knelt at Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun!

Chapter 2752: Zhou Xiuyun

No matter how cold and mature Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun pretended to be, they were teenagers anyway, and they could barely resist this dirty woman who happened to be a mother.

“Who… Who are you?”

After a long stun, Chu Zhiyun took a deep breath and tried to remain cold and calm. “Who else is inside? Only your daughter?”

“Yes, just me and my daughter, nobody else. We’ve been hungry for a long time. I could hold it back, but my daughter couldn’t anymore, so I came out to look for some food for her.”

The woman raised her hands pitifully. “Please spare us! Please spare my daughter!”

She staggered to the back of the cold room.

Holding their storm bolters and their daggers, Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun stared at her and pressed close.

The frozen seafood in the stinky cold room had already turned into dirty and repulsive fluids. No wonder the child was crying out aloud.

Hearing her mother’s voice, a little girl who was three years old at most crawled out from the back of a pile of frozen meat and laughed.

The girl looked entirely different from her mother. She could almost be called adorable even though she had spent quite a few days in a cold and damp cold room. The girl couldn’t talk much yet, and she did not know what kind of danger she was in. She simply crawled to her mother on all fours in delight.

The middle-aged woman hugged and kissed her daughter. Her tears ran out, but unwilling to scare the child, she tried to put on a smile and whispered to her daughter, “Good girl, it’s fine. I’ve found food for you. We’ll have food soon. Be a good girl. Don’t cry…”

“Mom…”

The girl stared at Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun, her eyes bulging. She did not know the dangers of the human world yet, and she only felt that their clothes were pretty. “Flower! Flower!”

“Help my daughter!”

The middle-aged woman begged again, “Please!”

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun both took a deep breath, trying to calm themselves down.

“Who are you exactly? Why are you hiding here? How long have you been hiding? What have you seen?”

Tang Ka waved his dagger at the woman and pretended to be scary. “Tell us everything!”

The little girl, however, was interested in the glittering toy. She giggled and reached her hand for the dagger.

Tang Ka immediately blushed. He laid the dagger down at a loss and did not know where he should put his hands.

Thankfully, the middle-aged woman did not notice Tang Ka’s panic, and she confessed her identity dutifully.

She said that her name was Zhou Xiuyun and her daughter’s name was Tiantian. Her husband was an officer in the garrison of the Thick Soil Sector, and she had come here with her husband.

Zhou Xiuyun was a woman with a lot of misfortunes. She was born in a family of Immortal Cultivators from a remote world of the Imperium of True Human Beings, but her spiritual root was not awakened. Also, her family went bankrupt as she grew up, making it impossible for her to find a suitable husband. She had married twice, but both her husbands were killed as they struggled to make a living.

But thankfully, the Immortal Cultivators were not superstitious. Also, although her spiritual root was not awakened, she still had her family genes, and there was still a good chance that her children could become Immortal Cultivators. Therefore, she soon married a third husband.

Her third husband was not a bad person, but his family was not very powerful enough, and he couldn’t be promoted despite the great contributions he made. He served in local troops for a dozen years but was still blocked by the glass ceiling.

As it happened, the expedition army announced that it wanted more soldiers, and the Sector Master of Zhou Xiuyun’s hometown established a half business and half colonization troop ambitiously, intending to share the results of victory. Experienced officers were in demand, so Zhou Xiuyun’s husband signed up for it.

Those officers were likely to spend the rest of their lives in the frontline, so most of the officers moved their whole families to the frontline. While the officers stayed in the military bases, their families became the citizens in the towns of civilians like New Joy City.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun knew what happened next without her telling them.

When the Covenant Alliance attacked, the garrison of the Tick Soil Sector barely put up any resistance. New Joy City was occupied three days later, and most civilians were captured and taken away.

However, the city was too huge, and there were too many buildings and sewers. It was not really surprising that some survivors escaped.

Zhou Xiuyun told Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun that she was an employee of this supermarket. When she heard explosions from the suburb, she hurried to find her daughter and tried to flee.

At that time, most civilians were trying to escape from the city, and the roads were completely jam-packed. Besides, Zhou Xiuyun broke her leg by accident on her way of escape. In a panic, she returned to the supermarket that she was most familiar with and hid in the cold room.

She had taken a lot of food from the shelves to the cold room, but in her panic, she forgot to bring water. Besides, it was impossible to dissolve formula and supplementary food with cold water. The child was also falling sick because of the terrible environment in the cold room. So, after a few days, she couldn’t hold it back anymore and decided to take her chances to go out for heaters and medicines. Then, she ran into Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun were both suspicious after hearing what she said.

“Even if we’re willing to let go of you, where can you go when this city has already been surrounded? Do you want to hide in this cold room forever?” asked Chu Zhiyun coldly, narrowing her eyes.

“No, no. I was planning to escape from the city after my leg has recovered.”

Zhou Xiuyun rolled her pants and showed them her leg injury. There was indeed the sign of a bone fracture, but it had been mostly healed thanks to the gels and cell stimulator on her leg.

Zhou Xiuyun told Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun that the Imperium built an enormous shelter outside of the city for emergencies and that the shelter contained enough resources for people to hide inside for decades.

She should’ve gone to the shelter a long time ago, but partly because she was crippled and partly because she didn’t know what was going on outside, she had postponed the operation.

“I’m telling you the truth! You can check this out if you don’t believe me!”

As she talked, Zhou Xiuyun took out a piece of delicate magic equipment that looked like a compass. “I have a map! A map that leads the way to the shelter!”

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun looked at each other. Tang Ka took the compass and examined it for a moment, but he didn’t know how to open it.

“Ah, it can’t be opened without my fingerprint. Only the fingerprints of the officer’s family members can open it.”

As Zhou Xiuyun spoke, she pressed the back of the compass with her middle finger. Immediately, a 3D light beam rose from the compass and displayed a detailed map of the town, where their location, as well as the optimal route to the shelter, had been marked.

A zigzagging red line would lead them out of this town to somewhere in the unoccupied area.

It was not very close, but not too far away either. One could reach the destination on foot as long as they had enough preparations.

If they could find a vehicle, it would be even easier. They could reach the destination within a day.

“What do we do?”

Tang Ka asked Chu Zhiyun in a low voice while Chu Zhiyun was staring at the glittering shelter on the map deep in thought.

“Monitor, aren’t we supposed to… report this to the mentors?”

Seeing that Chu Zhiyun was silent, Tang Ka ventured to ask again.

“No!”

Zhou Xiuyun raised her voice in panic. “Please let us go! We’re just civilians! We don’t know anything! We don’t know anything!”

“Mom! Mom!”

The cute baby grew nervous and burst into tears again when she saw that her mother was terrified and helpless.

With mysterious light flashing in her eyes, Chu Zhiyun gazed at the wailing child.

“How odd.”

Chu Zhiyun suddenly said to Tang Ka, “The children in the Covenant Alliance seem to have forgotten how to cry a long time ago.”

“Huh?”

Tang Ka was confused. “Monitor, what are you talking about?”

“Give me the map.”

Biting her lips, Chu Zhiyun said, “Let them stay in the cold room and give them the heaters and the medicines.”

“Well…”

Tang Ka hesitated. “I don’t think it’s the standard procedure of pursuing. What if we’re discovered?”

“Cut the crap. It has occurred to me that you’re growing more and more disobedient.”

Narrowing her eyes, Chu Zhiyun glared at Tang Ka dangerously. “What is the matter with you?”

Chapter 2753: Li Yao’S Fury!

“Fine, you’re the boss.”

Tang Ka mumbled a complaint and went for medicines and heaters.

However, they didn’t keep Zhou Xiuyun and her daughter in the cold room, mainly because the environment there was too horrible. It was possible that the little girl would really fall sick if she stayed any longer.

It was the first time that the little girl breathed in fresh air since half a month ago. She couldn’t be happier when she crawled and ran in the aisles in the supermarket. Her giggles could be heard throughout the place.

Although Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun had seen a lot of innocent kids from the movies of the Imperium, it still felt different when they saw one in the flesh.

As if they had encountered a creature that was entirely different from themselves, they were greatly curious about the girl named Tiantian.

After drinking up the formula that Chu Zhiyun made for her in person, Tiantian grew even more intimate with her. She kept climbing on Chu Zhiyun’s body and burying her head in Chu Zhiyun’s arms.

Chu Zhiyun appeared both awkward and confused, as if she were holding a fragile glass, before she smiled genuinely.

“Don’t climb on me. Be a good girl. Careful!”

She lowered her voice and clumsily talked to the little kid.

There was a special area in the supermarket dedicated to children. In this area, not only were all sorts of toys sold, but there were also a pool of ocean balls and a plastic castle, which became a paradise for Tiantian. She threw a ball at Tang Ka and then gave a toy apple to Chu Zhiyun while smelling it as if it were a real apple.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun were completely fascinated by the fairy.

Zhou Xiuyun had been observing them timidly, as if she were at their mercy. The poor woman did not really have a choice either.

The big kids and the little kid played for half a day until the sunset. Then, they realized the cruel reality as the air turned cold.

“Monitor, it’s time to make a decision.”

Looking at Tiantian who had finally fallen asleep in her mother’s arms after crazy fun, Tang Ka asked, “What… should we do?

“Tomorrow is the last day of the ultimate test.”

Chu Zhiyun said, upset, “Let’s wait until tomorrow.”

It was definitely not a good answer but bordered on a lie to themselves.

Tang Ka, however, understood Chu Zhiyun very well, because it was exactly what he had in mind too.

The longer he stayed with Tiantian, the less willing he was to give the ignorant child away to the holy temple where she would be transformed into someone like him.

Tang Ka then remembered the people that he saw in the movies, TV series, and commercials from the Imperium. Those people seemed healthy, clean, and happy. Even when they were in pain and sad, they could still unleash their feelings by roaring and crying without any concerns.

It seemed their lives were “realer” than the lives of those in the Covenant Alliance. Gradually lost in the tempting illusions, Tang Ka wondered whether he craved to become a Purger or a target to be purged.

“You will stay here tonight. Try to prepare more food and water-cleaning cartridges, got it?”

Squatting and petting Tiantian, Chu Zhiyun said to Zhou Xiuyun, “Tomorrow. We’ll make a decision tomorrow about what we will do!”

Zhou Xiuyun nodded quickly and shed dirty tears again. She said softly, “Thank you! Thank you! I knew that you’re good people!”

Good people?

Tang Ka was not sure.

According to the laws in the Covenant Alliance, they were probably the worst villains who had been enticed by extraterrestrial devils without knowing it.

With complicated feelings, they returned to their rendezvous point.

After six days of the “ultimate test”, half of the participants had been eliminated, and the remaining students all kept a distance from each other.

Everybody was deep in thought. Their faces and their eyes were covered in vague mists, as if everybody had got a secret.

After only six days, all the students seem to have been reborn.

Tang Ka thought to himself. I wonder what they came across in their purge missions. Did they encounter their ‘Zhou Xiuyun’ but they would rather not talk about it?

This enormous city had only been occupied for ten days, and it was natural that some survivors were still hiding. Tang Ka thought that it was a reasonable guess.

Liao Meng, however, was still staring at Tang Ka ruthlessly like a rabid dog that was going to bite someone.

It was rather uncanny that this guy was still not eliminated after two consecutive losses of self-control.

Everybody was particularly reticent on the way back to the camp, including Li Yao who was in Tang Ka’s head.

After the lights in the camp were turned off and Tang Ka closed his eyes, he couldn’t help but call Li Yao, “Grandpa Yao, are you still there? Why are you not talking? Also, I sense that your soul waves are very drastic. You seem very… angry. Is that my illusion?”

“No, it’s not. I’m indeed very angry, and I have to control myself so that my fury will not flood out of your pores and raze this camp to the ground,” said Li Yao coldly, one word after another.

“Well…”

Tang Ka was surprised. “Why?”

Although he didn’t know much about this mysterious Grandpa Yao, he had found Grandpa Yao cheerful and optimistic after a week of his company, as if the man was never really upset about anything.

“I’m angry for four reasons.”

Li Yao said, “Firstly, I sensed nobody in the cold room in the supermarket in the previous five days. Although I’m unable to sense things when I’m in your body, that woman who calls herself Zhou Xiuyun is not an Immortal Cultivator who can freely control her breath and heartbeat but an ordinary person, and I should’ve been able to hear her even though the wall of the cold room was thick.

“There can only be two explanations for that: either my senses were more weakened than I thought, or she never hid in there before today.”

“Well…”

Tang Ka’s eyes bulged, and he breathed hastily. “What do you mean? There was nobody in the cold room, and she didn’t hide there until today?”

“Secondly…”

Ignoring Tang Ka, Li Yao continued his analysis, “In case you didn’t notice, the very first thing that Tiantian had after she came out was the formula that your monitor made for her. I don’t know if you people can understand parental love, but in Imperium… or any places other than the Covenant Alliance, a real mother wouldn’t have asked someone else to make food for her daughter in such a scenario.

“Thirdly, do you remember what you were doing when Zhou Xiuyun bumped into the shelves? At that time, you were roaming in the supermarket with your monitor in the fancy clothes from the Imperium. You weren’t ‘purging’ anything at all.

“If you were Zhou Xiuyun, a woman who secretly ran out to get hot water and medicines for your child after hiding in a cold room for half a month, only to accidentally see two teenagers who were dressed like the natives of the Imperium, what would you think?

“Would you think that those teenagers were civilians hiding in the supermarket just like you, or would you think that the teenagers were whimsically teaching themselves to be ‘Lurkers’ while they were taking part in an ‘ultimate test’?

“Don’t forget that woman’s self-introduction. She claimed to be a family member of an officer in the local garrison, so she couldn’t have been completely ignorant of the Covenant Alliance. She knew the classic look of the Covenant Alliance people, which was nothing like how you appeared!

“But what did she say when she met you? ‘Please don’t kill us’. In other words, even though you were not wearing the standard uniform of the Covenant Alliance, and you obviously showed that you had feelings, she did not mistake you for two civilians of the Imperium at all. Why?”

Tang Ka’s heart was frozen at first, before it was shattered and absorbed into a bottomless hole.

“What’s that supposed to mean?”

The young man was almost crying. “That’s just your speculation, right? You don’t have any proof. What if she were only too nervous?”

“Fourthly, the most important proof…”

Li Yao sighed and said, “The map that she took out and the special shelter that she talked about are lies. That place does not exist at all. It’s only a trap for you.”

“How do you know?”

Tang Ka blurted out, “How do you know that the map is fake?”

“Because I’m in possession of the coordinates of all the real military shelters on this planet.”

Li Yao said casually, “And the shelter that this ‘Zhou Xiuyun’ mentioned is not among them.”

Backed by the whole Imperium of True Human Beings, it was not a problem for Li Yao to get a detailed list and description of the military bases and shelters on this planet either through General Lei Chenghu, Empress Dowager Li Linghai, or through the young emperor himself.

Chapter 2754: Codename Fuxi

“So, Zhou Xiuyun is fake?”

Tang Ka mumbled, “You’re saying that she’s not a refugee from the Imperium but a native of the Covenant Alliance just like us, or even… a ‘Lurker’ who can simulate feelings vividly?”

“I don’t know the real identity of this Zhou Xiuyun. What I’m interested in is the little girl named Tiantian who’s supposed to be her daughter.”

Li Yao’s voice was even colder. His every word seemed to be covered in thick ice, but no matter how thick the ice was, the hotness behind it could not be sealed. Sparks were darting out of Li Yao’s soul. “Assuming that Zhou Xiuyun is really a Lurker of the Covenant Alliance, what about Tiantian? Why is she so close to Zhou Xiuyun and really regards Zhou Xiuyun as her mother? Such a little kid can’t lie, can she?

“I’ve come up with several possibilities.

“Firstly, ‘Zhou Xiuyun’ is real. I mean, there is really an unfortunate woman with this name who came to the frontline from the inland of the Imperium with the hope for a new life, only to be captured by the Covenant Alliance during an attack.

“Because this woman’s identity is very suitable, her memories and her feelings, including her love for her daughter, were ‘extracted’ as a ‘template’ and applied to some of the Lurkers, so that those Lurkers could imitate Zhou Xiuyun so vividly that even her own daughter can’t tell the difference.

“In other words, the ‘Zhou Xiuyun’ we saw was fake, but her daughter Tiantian was real!”

“Well…”

Tang Ka was even more shocked.

This evil and unbelievable possibility was far beyond his imagination.

“The second possibility is that this Tiantian is truly the daughter of a Lurker named ‘Zhou Xiuyun’, and she was born to be ‘props’ for her mother to win other people’s trust. After all, a middle-aged woman could be suspicious, but who would question an ignorant toddler?

“Furthermore, didn’t you say that the Purgers were groomed since they were very little? That’s why you’re known as ‘Children of Holy Light’. It’s very possible that this Tiantian was destined to become a Lurker in the future when she was still a fetus, isn’t it?”

Tang Ka took a deep breath and didn’t know what to say.

“The third possibility.”

Li Yao paused and was silent for a long time, as if he had to calm himself down before he could talk about this cold possibility. “Tiantian is the real Zhou Xiuyun’s daughter, but there was no time for the Lurkers to imitate her mother so vividly that she couldn’t tell the difference. Therefore, through medications or brain surgeries, they changed Tiantian’s cognition system and made Tiantian think that the Lurker was her mother!

“Okay, these are the only three possibilities I can think of. Tang Ka, which of the three possibilities do you think is most likely, and which one is evilest?”

“I… Grandpa Yao…”

Tang Ka gritted his teeth but still shed tears in silence. He mumbled, “How can it happen? How can they do that? How can the world be like this?”

“I’ve asked myself the same questions again and again, but I couldn’t find an answer.”

Li Yao sneered, “However, I found out later that it was unnecessary to seek answers to those questions. What I only need to do is to change this world and make the scumbags who created those miseries pay!”

“Grandpa Yao…”

Tang Ka was surprised at the surging tides that Li Yao raised in his head. They were a hundred times more powerful than what he used to know.

Tang Ka finally believed what Li Yao said casually before about how he only carried out 1% of his power and how he could raze the camp to the ground with a sneeze!

What was this guy exactly? The king of extraterrestrial devils?

However, if the gods did atrocious things such as manipulating the feelings of a three-year-old, would those on the gods’ opposite side be really evil?

“I thought that the Imperium of True Human Beings was the evilest country in the universe because all the kind-hearted people in that bloody country of Immortal Cultivators were being destroyed mercilessly.”

Li Yao said solemnly, “But after I’ve seen the Covenant Alliance in person, it finally occurs to me that not just kind-hearted or evil people, but humanity itself is being destroyed. Everybody is manipulated and treated as a tool or a weapon. This is probably the evilest thing one can imagine!

“Human beings can be killed, but the feelings and self-will of human beings shouldn’t be trodden upon. That is my boundary and why I’m so furious today!”

“Well…”

Taking a deep breath, Tang Ka asked, “What are you going to do, Grandpa Yao? My monitor and I will definitely be on your side.”

“Even if it means resisting your gods?”

Li Yao finally smiled.

“Well…”

Tang Ka was speechless for a moment. He thought for a long time and said, “Who knows what the gods really are and what their will is?”

“Ha, you’ve finally woken up. That’s right. My purpose is exactly to figure out what the ‘gods’ and the ‘Ultimate Benevolence Masters’ who created the Covenant Alliance and all these tragedies are. That they are made of the last drops of blood of the gods from the primeval war is utter bulls*it!”

Nobody knew the history of the Covenant Alliance more clearly than Li Yao did. With Wuying Qi 3.0’s memories, he knew perfectly that the Covenant Alliance was created by none other than Wuying Qi himself at the beginning.

However, Wuying Qi did nothing more than activating the crystal processor system in the man-manufacturing factory that he accidentally found and inputing a few “backdoors” and secret instructions into this system.

Wuying Qi’s original purpose was to get a batch of subordinates that were absolutely loyal to him. He never thought to create the Covenant Alliance.

So, it was impossible for Wuying Qi to create five super warriors that were particularly strong and name them “Ultimate Benevolence Masters”.

However, there were too many uncertainties in the war and conflicts later, and some of the manufactured men somehow awakened their absolute loyalty to the Pangu Clan and controlled the crystal processor system of the man-manufacturing factory.

According to Wuying Qi 3.0, that crystal processor system was broken and deeply flawed in the first place. Nobody could tell what happened when this system was running.

Li Yao also remembered that Wuying Qi 3.0 said that the super crystal processor system in the man-manufacturing factory was known as “Fuxi”.

Li Yao asked Long Yangjun later if she knew this super system named Fuxi.

Long Yangjun nodded and told Li Yao that the Fuxi system was once the greatest invention of the primeval civilizations and was the cornerstone for the Pangu alliance.

It was a unified super system that combined the computation systems of the thirteen carbon-based species.

Any carbon-based civilization would boast a set of advanced tools for calculation when it was developed to the point that it could leave its mother planet.

Because their power and their architecture were different, those tools varied greatly in terms of name and appearance.

It was not hard to imagine what a complicated and difficult job it was to connect all those different computers so that they could work together in a compatible manner.

However, in order for the thirteen carbon-based species to completely assimilate and share each other’s history, memories, and feelings, this job had to be completed no matter how great its price was!

Long Yangjun told Li Yao that the whole Pangu alliance worked for that purpose for ten thousand years. Over the ten thousand years, countless sages were devoted to the glorious cause with their lives as fuel. Unimaginable difficulties and barriers were overcome. Eventually, all the information and computation ability in the Pangu universe were gathered into the super unified computation system named Fuxi!

It was exactly because of Fuxi that the thirteen carbon-based species whose living environment and whose customs were entirely different could live in peace and share their wisdom and courage with each other, thereby continuing their civilization for hundreds of thousands of years.

By Long Yangjun’s estimation, even the Pangu alliance’s exploration beyond the black wall couldn’t have been completed without the help of the Fuxi system.

However, good days didn’t last long. Li Yao roughly guessed what happened later based on information from various sources. After the Pangu civilization was horrified by the views behind the black wall and the unknown “Flood”, they ran back to their home and emasculated themselves by destroying tremendous technologies on space sailing and crystal processors. As a result, the crystal processors of the primeval age that mankind excavated today were not much more advanced than what mankind currently had. They were almost on the same level.

The first thing to be excised was, undoubtedly, the Fuxi system.

Chapter 2755: A Real Man

The Pangu Alliance was concerned about the Fuxi system for mainly two reasons.

Firstly, the Fuxi could help a super fleet jump beyond the black wall or even send out signals, which would likely expose the secrets of the Pangu universe and attract the attention of the Flood.

Although the Fuxi system was controlled by the leaders of the Pangu alliance, who were visionary, wise, and rational, nobody could guarantee that an insane and crazy lunatic wouldn’t do something like hijacking the whole system and destroying the Pangu universe. If history were of any reference, there were always such lunatics.

Secondly, the Fuxi system, which stored almost all the information in the Pangu universe, had become more and more mysterious and unpredictable. All signs indicated that subtle and dangerous ripples were arising within the system.

Just like how mankind nowadays was worried about the rise of artificial intelligence, the Pangu civilization was faced with the same problem at the crossroad of their fate.

The two concerns eventually led to one decision, which was the Pangu alliance’s self-emasculation. They destroyed their most powerful fleets, abandoned the advanced crystal processor technologies, and sealed 90% of the information and functions in the Fuxi system.

Of course, it was absolutely impossible to completely destroy the Fuxi system.

As Professor Mo Xuan once said, it was impossible for human beings to live without the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus today, so it was only a matter of time that new forms of life were born.

By the same logic, the Pangu alliance couldn’t have united thirteen entirely different carbon-based species and spread throughout the universe without the advanced crystal processor system.

If they completely lost their Spiritual Nexus and their crystal processors, the whole alliance would fall apart, and every planet would turn into a lone island in the vast sea of stars, shivering in the darkness until it perished.

Nobody would hear their last wail in the cold vacuum when the Spiritual Nexus was gone.

Therefore, the Pangu alliance kept 10% of the information and functions of the Fuxi. It was an emasculated version of Fuxi.

Later, the Pangu civilization emasculated their feelings and emotions with the help of the emasculated version of Fuxi. Without an advanced computation system, it was impossible to lock one’s brain, melt genes, or conduct artificial reproduction.

However, most of the files on the role of the emasculated version of the Fuxi system were lost in the primeval war, and it was impossible to tell what it was.

Li Yao only heard from Long Yangjun that Pangu and Nuwa both likely had part of the control permissions of the emasculated version of the Fuxi system because Pangu was the leader of the alliance and Nuwa was creating human beings with the Fuxi system.

When the war reached the most critical moment, they had definitely forgotten all their concerns. Maybe the Nuwa Clan tried to restore all the functions of the Fuxi, forcing the Pangu Clan to do the same. Or maybe, the ultimate destruction of Pangu, Nuwa, and other primeval clans was exactly due to the Fuxi system?

After all, it was impossible for human beings, who were still weak back then, to defeat so many formidable species all at once.

It was impossible to find out the truth anymore after hundreds of thousands of years, but Li Yao was certain of one thing.

Whether the crystal processors in the Covenant Alliance were excavated from relics or captured from the Imperium, it was some incomplete version of the Fuxi that was running on those crystal processors.

The Covenant Alliance was much more dependent on the Spiritual Nexus than the federation and the Imperium were. It was exactly because of the stability and reliability of the Fuxi that they were able to pass the orders of the Ultimate Benevolence Masters among hundreds of Sectors in some sort of telepathy.

What were the Ultimate Benevolence Masters exactly? How did they learn to use the Fuxi? Was there a deeper reason for the birth of the Covenant Alliance besides the accidental trigger of Wuying Qi 3.0?

Those questions had popped up after Li Yao observed the Covenant Alliance in person for a couple of days.

“Take a good rest, kid. I’m going back and take a rest too. We’ll be… very busy tomorrow,” Li Yao whispered.

“Grandpa Yao, what’s on your mind? Go on a killing spree?”

Tang Ka grew excited and couldn’t help but clench his fists.

“Everybody familiar with me knows that I’m a reasonable man who doesn’t like violence. I believe that the most important thing for human beings is communication, and all the problems can be solved by talking in peace. So, I don’t like violent solutions unless there are no other choices. People often mock me for that!”

Li Yao said, “Besides, you, your monitor, and Tiantian are involved in this. If that woman is a fake ‘Zhou Xiuyun’, Tiantian would be innocent. Even if Tiantian is a tool that has been modified, she’s still little and can be rescued.

“Maybe I can’t save everybody on this planet, but I should at least try to save those I’ve met.

“Just listen to me tomorrow, and I’ll try to send you, your monitor, and Tiantian out of the city to a real shelter where you will be basically safe. The Covenant Alliance does not have enough troops to eliminate all the underground shelters on this planet, which is not the real target for your leaders.

“Trust me, I will definitely show up before you and let you see the real sunlight within a year!”

“Grandpa Yao… Thank you!”

Tang Ka was both grateful and confused. “But if this ultimate test is really a special experiment, a lot of people must be watching us in secret, or at least watching over my monitor. How can we escape?”

“Very simple.”

Li Yao smiled. “The woman named Zhou Xiuyun gave you a detailed map, didn’t she? A lot of people must be waiting to capture you at the destination marked on the map. So, as long as we go exactly the opposite way, our odds of escape will be very high.

“If everything goes well, it’s possible that you will reach a safe, warm, and secret shelter by tomorrow night.”

“Well…”

After a long hesitation, Tang Ka still asked, “What if it doesn’t go well?”

Li Yao’s soul ripples were sharp and bloody, like the teeth of a shark.

“That shouldn’t be your concern.”

Li Yao smiled and said, “That’s a problem that your mentors, your director, and everybody in this purge troop should be worried about.”

…

When Li Yao’s soul returned to his shell, he found himself lying in the middle of a steamer. The neural interaction fluids and the high-energy nutrition fluids that he was soaked in were all gone, and the steamer, which used to be the cocoon launch cabin, was deformed and cracked.

Through the cracks in the cabin, Li Yao could hear the earsplitting alarms and see that Xiaoming and Wenwen were busy controlling plenty of spiritual puppets.

Li Yao tried to push, but he couldn’t open the seriously deformed cover of the cabin at all. He simply kicked it away and rose from the cabin.

“Why?”

Li Yao frowned and looked at the messy environment. “Why does it seem that a great explosion just took place?”

“Dad!”

Wenwen burst into tears of joy. “Are you alright?”

“Dad, didn’t you cause all this?”

Xiaoming declared in rage, “Just now, your body suddenly went out of control. As if all your cells were burning crazily, your body temporarily soared and vaporized all the fluids in the cabin. The power your body unleashed even broke the launch cabin. Wenwen and I tried our best to maintain the stability of soul connection. What exactly did you do just now?”

“So serious?”

Li Yao scratched his hair. “It seems that the relation between the soul and the shell is still mysterious, and they can’t be completely separated even though I’m in the Divinity Branching Stage. Nothing really happened. I just need to take a good rest. Also, would you please fix the launch cabin as soon as possible? I have to use it again later.

“Right, prepare twenty dosages of brain simulator and inject it into the neural interaction fluids. Okay, I’m going to take a shower and have some food!”

“Hold on a second…”

Xiaoming said suspiciously, “Why do you want so much brain simulator? The current neural interaction fluids are very stable as they are, and while the brain stimulator can significantly improve the activity of your brain cells, it’s very unstable and has many unknown dangers and side effects!

“Hey, Dad, you’re not taking action the moment you see injustice again, are you? Don’t lie to us. We can easily tell that it’s true!”

“Come on!”

Furrowing her tiny eyebrows, Wenwen pouted out her lips and said, “Dad, we know that you like playing a hero, but things are different this time. This time, you’re swinging to millions of lightyears away in the middle of billions of enemies with nothing but your own soul. Also, don’t forget that you still have a mission!”

“I know.”

Li Yao said rather embarrassedly, “Hehe, hehe.”

“What do you mean by ‘hehe’?”

Xiaoming asked further, “Are you playing a hero or not? Forget that I asked. I can tell from your eyes and your smile that you are! You’re hundreds of years old, but why are you still so rash?”

“First of all, I’m only more than a hundred years old, and I’m even younger, subtracting the time I spent in hibernation. I’m still a vigorous young man as a Cultivator. Thank you.”

Li Yao rubbed his nose and said casually, “Secondly, your dad has been like this since his childhood, and it’s impossible for him to change his nature.. It can’t be helped. I’m just such a passionate and hotblooded man!”

Chapter 2756: Uncontrollable Folly

The next morning, the drizzle turned into rain, and the whole planet was enshrouded in a misty.

Such a misty and rainy day was the best day for killing.

At five o’clock in local time, the camp of the Children of Holy Light was already ablaze with lights. They had all got up for their daily training.

It was the last day of their test.

Having weathered through six days against the temptations of the extraterrestrial devils, they were all the distinguished students of the Holy Light Academy, or at least they thought so.

After one more day, they would become real purgers, which was one of the most important forces in the Covenant Alliance.

Everybody was excited, earnest, and hopeful. Those teenagers were so fascinated by their vision for the future that they did not realize that their excitement and their persistence in their dream had violated the rules of the Covenant Alliance.

At six o’clock in the morning, it was the time for morning prayer.

As if to celebrate the completion of the ultimate test, more mentors and even people more important than mentors had come to the camp today. Those people were all observing the ignorant teenagers thoughtfully from the shadows with rigid smiles on their faces.

Not far away, the purge troop was more active than before too. Now and then, supersonic shuttles were flying above their heads, making the metal tents clatter.

The enormous floating firepower platform had descended to only a hundred meters from the ground. It was so lower that the teenagers could see the glittering cannons on the platform. They even felt that the fortress would fall and crumble everything on the ground into smithereens.

The mentors told the young men that they would be admitted by the purge troop and receive training on this airborne fortress if they could pass the ultimate test.

All delighted, the teenagers embarked on the final part of their journey after breakfast.

At seven in the morning, hundreds of young men and women were dispersed in various areas of the city.

The rain grew heavier, but the mist was not driven away by the rain. Everything was so pale that it did not look like the real world at all.

Just like the day when someone defected for the first time, the rain and the fog gave the teenagers an illusionary sense of security, as if they would find a new path as long as they unveiled the rain and the mist.

At eight in the morning, all the young people were devoted to the purge operation, or so they appeared.

At nine, however, a minor accident took place in a supermarket in the south of the city.

Huala! Huala! Huala! Huala!

Bam! Bam! Bam!

Boom! Boom!

Someone shook the iron fences and hit the gate. Then, they blew up the gate into pieces with a magnetic bomb.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

As boots rumbled on the floor, two mentors and a team of soldiers from the purge troop barged in, fully armed. The person leading the way for them was exactly Liao Meng, the deputy monitor in Tang Ka’s class!

“This is the place! Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka have been doing things stealthily here since days ago. I even heard the laughs and cries of a baby yesterday. A very little one. They’re hiding a baby in this place!”

Liao Meng’s face was greasy, and his voice was high-pitched, which seemed rather unfitting for someone of his body size.

“I suspect that they’re possessed by extraterrestrial devils. A few days ago, I even saw colorful lights from the supermarket. God knows what they were doing!”

While declaring ruthlessly, Liao Meng led the mentors and the soldiers into the deepest part of the supermarket.

They rushed very fast and reached the entertainment area in the supermarket only ten seconds later.

What they saw, however, dumbfounded Liao Meng, who found it hard to believe his eyes.

Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka were not half as panicked as he thought they would be. They were not in clothes they weren’t supposed to wear, or watching or listening to anything that they weren’t supposed to enjoy. On the contrary, the two of them had piled up tremendous projects, albums, and jade chips and were shattering and burning those things up according to the standard procedure of purging without even taking off their masks.

Tang Ka had set the power of his shatter gun to the maximum, which filled the room with deafening noises. It explained why they didn’t hear the strangers knocking on the door.

They were as calm as before even though they were surrounded by tremendous mentors and Purgers. Finishing the destruction work unhurriedly, they took off their masks and looked at everyone expressionlessly.

“How… How is this possible?”

Confused, Liao Meng mumbled, “Where is the baby?”

“What baby?”

Chu Zhiyun stared at her mentor calmly without looking at Liao Meng at all.

“Liao Meng said that he heard a baby’s cries from here yesterday,” her mentor explained.

“Oh.”

Not bothering why Liao Meng eavesdropped on her from outside, Chu Zhiyun said casually, “We destroyed a batch of toys yesterday. Some of them could laugh and cry like babies. The noises were probably from them when they were accidentally activated during burning. Is there a problem?”

“That is impossible!”

Liao Meng shouted, “I also saw colorful lights in the supermarket through the window. How do you explain that?”

“When we burned the stuff that contained tremendous impurities, a lot of light would naturally be caused. Is that really a problem?”

Chu Zhiyun was still expressionless without even frowning, as if she felt that Liao Meng’s stupid questions were a waste of her time.

“Then, why would you lock the door if you weren’t doing anything clandestine in this supermarket?”

Liao Meng’s face became dark.

“There are many beasts in the city. Some are even biochemical beasts whose genes have been strengthened.”

Chu Zhiyun said, “There may be battle puppets that can automatically search for and attack the enemy, and they may still be functioning after half a month.

“We do not want to attract their attention. If they notice us, the locked gate will buy us more time.”

Liao Meng was rendered speechless.

At this moment, the soldiers who searched the whole supermarket reported that nothing inappropriate was found. There were marks of burning in the warehouse and the cold room, but it was part of the standard procedure.

However, those places were still very hot, as if the incineration work had just been completed.

“Look, that is evidence!”

Liao Meng cried again, “You’ve just burned a lot of things! Why? What were you trying to destroy and disguise?”

“Today is the last day of our ultimate test. Everybody wants to complete more work so that they can receive more points.”

Chu Zhiyun explained unhurriedly again, “Everybody is trying their best to shatter and burn as many things as possible, but you’ve brought mentors and Purgers here and disregarded your own mission. Mr. Liao Meng, what’s your problem?”

“I… I… You… You…!”

Liao Meng’s dark face turned red and then pale. “I saw it! I heard it! You… You must be hiding them somewhere! Something is wrong! Something is definitely wrong! You can’t trick me!”

“I don’t know what you’re talking about, but I do know that one might see all kinds of illusions when their optical and auditory nerves are controlled by extraterrestrial devils.”

Pausing for a moment, Chu Zhiyun finally glanced at Liao Meng. “Mr. Liao Meng, are you sure that this folly and this waste of precious time and resources are not because of something you imagined?”

“You… Nonsense!”

Liao Meng burst into rage. “My loyalty to the gods is incomparable. You’re the ones who are possessed by extraterrestrial devils. You!”

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

His portable crystal processor beeped and flashed dangerous red light again.

“This is the third time you’ve lost control of your feelings, Mr. Liao Meng.”

Chu Zhiyun stroked her hair and said casually, “And you exceeded the warning line by a lot every time. If I recall correctly, you should be banished from this test after three losses of control.”

As if hit by a lightning bolt, Liao Meng was dumbfounded.

“Impossible. That’s impossible… That’s impossible… That’s impossible…”

He breathed more and more quickly, and the pimples on his face seemed to be exploding. His face was full of fury, fear, and desperation as he shouted, “I’m a Purger. My loyalty to the gods is incomparable. How can I lose control of myself? I can’t! It’s just your trick!”

“If you’re truly loyal to the gods, I suggest you have your brain thoroughly examined. I believe that the gods will definitely save you, Mr. Liao Meng,” Chu Zhiyun said coldly and turned to the mentors.

“Mentors, may we continue our purge work? We have a lot more things to do today, and we do not want to be disturbed by such trivia.”

Chapter 2757: The Path Of Your Own!

The two mentors who almost looked identical nodded at the same time. One of the mentors scanned Liao Meng’s face with blue light emanating from his eyes.

Oddly enough, under his eyes, the veins on Liao Meng’s face all bulged, and the man seemed to be wearing a bloody mask.

Now, everybody could see that Liao Meng’s boiling blood was running all over his body and his face.

“Mr. Liao Meng, you’re out,” said the mentor with blue eyes coldly.

“I’m out?”

It was not until ten seconds later that Liao Meng finally realized what it meant. His pupils constricted as he stared at Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka.

For a moment, Tang Ka even suspected that Liao Meng would lunge at him.

But eventually, Liao Meng simply jumped back after a scream, trying to escape from the supermarket.

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

His portable crystal processor shrieked. It was like a burning shackle that was restraining a rash and furious soul.

“I can’t be out! I’ve always been faithful to the gods! I…”

He jumped and wailed like a brute. He was about to run out of the supermarket when two soldiers of the purge troop knocked him down with a stun gun. He fell into the goods on the ground.

While the stun gun was not a lethal weapon, it was enough to make a stallion unconscious.

But Liao Meng got back to his feet with astonishing willpower.

His hair was erect, and his eyes became completely white. As if he couldn’t see what was around him, he reached out his hands, whose skin had been broken, hoping to run to where the fresh wind was coming.

Naturally, he fell again, and he was hauled back by two extensible chains, leaving a devastating trace of blood on the ground.

“This is impossible! I don’t believe it! Something must be wrong!”

In excruciating pain, Liao Meng shed tears of blood and cried, “I’m absolutely loyal to the Ultimate Benevolence Masters and the gods! I’m a Purger! I can’t be possessed by extraterritorial devils! You’ve all been tricked! You’re wrong! Help me! Help me, gods! Ahhhhhhhh!”

He writhed on the ground in his blood.

“The gods are helping you, so please stay quiet.”

The mentor with blue eyes squatted and pointed his finger at the center of Liao Meng’s forehead.

Liao Meng suddenly cramped, and noises resembling those when gears were stuck came from his throat. He was suddenly rigidified as if he were a dead body.

The mentor with blue eyes asked the soldiers to pull the half-dead Liao Meng away from the supermarket and left with them.

The other mentor, whose nose was particularly huge, stared at Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka.

He suddenly approached them and sniffed them carefully with his long and pointy nose as if he were a hound on its feet.

He sniffed Chu Zhiyun for a whole minute and then Tang Ka for another half.

“Ms. Chu Zhiyun, you’ve been the most distinguished student of the Holy Light Academy for the past three years, and you gave an excellent performance in this ultimate test. We look forward to your achievements after you become a real Purger,” said the hound-like mentor with a vague smile.

It was a compliment, but there was no warmth in his words at all.

Then, he moved his eyes that looked as if they were made of glass to Tang Ka. “As for you, Mr. Tang Ka, you reek of fear. Why? It’s absolutely unnecessary. Your improvement in the ultimate test is beyond everyone’s expectations. Remember, the gods are watching you and guiding you. As long as you obey the gods from the bottom of your heart, you should never fear.

“Alright, please resume your test. We’ll reveal the final answer at the camp at sunset.”

The hound-like mentor sniffed again and stepped back until he left the supermarket.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun both collapsed when the unannounced guests were away.

This time, even Chu Zhiyun who always seemed as cold as ice was sweating hard. She laid her hands on Tang Ka’s shoulder and could barely stand straight.

“That was close.”

Panting for a long time, Chu Zhiyun stared at the door of the supermarket and said gloomily, “Tang Ka, how did you know that danger was coming?”

“I… I don’t know. It’s just my guts. I’ve been feeling anxious since last night.”

Tang Ka said, “Didn’t you say that the most important thing for a Purger is ‘instinct’?”

“Good boy. It seems that the mentor was not wrong. You’ve really grown a lot in the past few days!”

Chu Zhiyun held Tang Ka’s arm and whispered, “I may have to respect your opinions more in the future.”

A smile flashed in Tang Ka’s face, and he said, “But you want to become a Lurker, and I’ll become a Purger at best. No, it’s possible that neither of us can become what we want to be, and we will end up exactly like Liao Meng.”

Chu Zhiyun was silent again.

“What do we do now then?”

The girl said in confusion, “I don’t want to go back. I don’t want to become like Liao Meng or any of those mentors. Have you not found that all of our mentors feel identical although they have different faces and body shapes? They’re like the different copies of the same template.

“If I’m destined to become one of the billions of copies of the same template, what’s the point of my life?”

“You want to run, right, monitor?”

Summoning his courage, Tang Ka said, “You’ve been meaning to run since we discovered Zhou Xiuyun and Tiantian yesterday.”

“I…”

Both determination and confusion were flashing in Chu Zhiyun’s eyes. She took a deep breath and mumbled, “I really don’t know what I want to do.

“It’s quite funny. Before the ultimate test, I was confident that I could train myself into a real Lurker against all the temptations from the extraterrestrial devils, but as time goes by, and maybe because extraterrestrial devils got into me, I’m less enthusiastic to become a Lurker now.

“I… I don’t know what I want exactly. But I know that I can’t tolerate my life in the past. I can’t stand myself becoming something like our mentors or our director.

“Run… Can I really run out of here? Tang Ka, what would you do?”

Chu Zhiyun loosened Tang Ka’s arm and walked absentmindedly. Then, she stepped on something hard.

She picked it up, only to see that it was an emblem of the Holy Light Academy with obvious scratches and blood on it.

It was Liao Meng’s badge. He accidentally dropped it when he writhed a moment ago.

“Let’s make this badge decide everything!”

Chu Zhiyun gritted her teeth and said, “If it’s heads, I’ll finish the purge operation and return to the camp; if it’s tails, I’ll… walk on a path of my own!”

She took a deep breath and flipped the badge, which hummed in midair.

Her eyes moved following the badge, but the badge was suddenly grabbed by a fist before it hit the ground.

Tang Ka, the owner of this fist, carefully placed the badge on the ground with the tails facing up, before he put on the most brilliant smile.

“Look, monitor, it’s tails.”

Tang Ka said, “So, it’s already decided. Let’s go!”

“Tang Ka, you…”

Chu Zhiyun was at a loss, feeling that she didn’t know her “sidekick” that grew up with her anymore. “You’re leaving with me? Tang Ka, do you know the consequences?”

“Consequences? To be purged in the holy temples at worst. Will it be any different from right now?”

Tang Ka picked up her hand and said softly and firmly, “You were the first person I saw after I opened my eyes. We’ve been together for so many years. So, you will never leave me alone, will you?”

“Well…”

Confusion flowed out of Chu Zhiyun’s eyes again. She thought for a moment and nodded firmly. “That’s right. As long as you’re willing to follow me, I will never abandon you. Let’s go!”

The two kids stared at each other for a long time, until they were both smiling. They clenched their arms and strode forward.

“Tang Ka, will the gods still watch over us?” Chu Zhiyun asked when they were about to leave the supermarket.

“Well, I don’t know if the gods will watch over us.”

Tang Ka waved his fist and said, “But I’m sure that a certain someone will!”

Chapter 2758: Manliness!

Each carrying a bagful of plastic items that couldn't be burnt indoors, they moved them to an empty place on the street, pretending that they would burn the items in the open air, while they were actually scanning the environment.

The town was still covered in mist, and nothing could be heard except for the rain. The noises of boots and tanks were all drifting away.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun looked at each other and made up their minds. They took off their masks simultaneously.

Quietly walking to a collapsed wall at the corner of the street, they carefully moved a cement block away, revealing the remains of a shuttle down below.

Tang Ka curled his finger and knocked the dented shell of the shuttle three times. "Du, du, du."

Then, he clenched his fists and bashed the shell twice. "Dum! Dum!"

After a long silence, the back cover of the broken shuttle was slowly opened, revealing two devastated people who were coughing hard.

"I think Tiantian is having a fever."

Zhou Xiuyun hugged her daughter so hard as if she were trying to melt her daughter into her own body. She said in a panic, "It's raining outside. What do we do?"

Tang Ka laid his hand on Tiantian's forehead and cried, "She's burning!"

He looked at Tiantian, only to see that her eyebrows were furrowed and her face was red. She was not cheerful or as merry as yesterday.

"If she has a fever, give her some medicines. We have to leave right now!"

Chu Zhiyun said coldly, "If nobody takes you out today, you won't be able to leave tomorrow!"

"What?"

Zhou Xiuyun burst into tears of joy. "You're leaving with us? That's… That's fantastic!"

"Enough talking. Let's go!"

Chu Zhiyun covered Tiantian with a blanket before she moved out the big bag that she and Tang Ka had hidden in the shuttle earlier.

There was no turning back now that they made this step. Both of them looked back at the camp and embarked on the path of escape with Zhou Xiuyun and Tiantian.

"This way! The map shows this way!"

Tang Ka, who was holding the "compass", led the way. Zhou Xiuyun was in the middle with Tiantian, and Chu Zhiyun was covering up for them with two storm bolters. Weapons were not unusual in New Joy City even though it was a town of civilians, and Chu Zhiyun had picked up some of the guns in case of danger.

They gradually reached the depths of the city. The place seemed absolutely quiet and empty, but it also felt that countless ferocious beasts were staring at them from behind the mist while holding their breaths.

"We should be reaching the northern suburb of New Joy City soon. As far as I know, it's an agricultural area, so no purge operations were arranged there."

Chu Zhiyun whispered, "We should be safe as soon as we make it there."

"Do you hear it, Tiantian?"

Zhou Xiuyun said to her daughter in a low voice, "We'll be safe soon. Very soon."

Her lips curved into a smile.

Tang Ka, however, came to a sudden halt. He raised his wrist high and started moving randomly.

"What's wrong?"

Chu Zhiyun asked in a stun, "Why did you stop walking?"

"I lost the way."

Tang Ka mumbled, "I can't seem to find the way out of here."

"That is impossible!"

Zhou Xiuyun grew anxious before Chu Zhiyun said anything. "There's a route on the map and even automatic navigation. All you need to do is to follow the arrow on the map!"

"I know, but the map is stuck. It must've crashed. Look!"

Tang Ka waved the compass in his hand, and the arrow on it was indeed dead. "The signals must've been blocked by all these skyscrapers and the bad weather. I need to find a place that's empty and high to search for signals…"

Tang Ka looked around and climbed many high walls and pillars, as if he were really searching for signals.

Chu Zhiyun and Zhou Xiuyun could only follow him, praying that they wouldn't bump into anyone.

They did seem quite lucky, as they ran into neither their classmates nor the patrolling soldiers of the purge troop on their journey.

However, they seemed to have used up their good luck. Right after Tang Ka climbed on a wall that wasn't too high, with Chu Zhiyun as well as Zhou Xiuyun following him, a deafening explosion burst out not far away from their feet, not only careening the wall but also causing a big hole in the ground.

It was a landmine or a bomb on the road!

It was a common trick to plant tremendous landmines and bombs on the road before evacuating from a valuable area.

Speaking of which, it was actually the Covenant Alliance that played the trick first. In the past twenty years, although the Imperium had been retreating, they had turned most of the worlds they gave up into fatal swamps with landmines and bombs.

Those weapons were shabby and unattractive, but they could be a major headache for experts and regular soldiers.

The Covenant Alliance's landmines and bombs left a deep impression on the Imperium, and now that the Imperium had retreated, they naturally left some "gifts" for the Covenant Alliance.

Tang Ka had probably triggered a bomb by accident and raised a rather remarkable explosion.

Maybe it was a coincidence, and maybe it was on purpose, but below these ruins was the congregation point of several sewers. It was actually hollow below the road that was only several meters thick.

After the road collapsed, they fell onto nothing all the way into the sewers.

After a night of rain, a lot of water had been gathered in the sewers, which were almost entirely full.

Some of the sewers had also been broken or blocked in the previous battle, making the water in the unblocked sewers even more rapid.

Putong!

When the four of them fell into the sewers, they felt that they had just dropped from a waterfall. They couldn't tell the direction at all, and enormous attraction forces were pulling them to the bottom of the sewers like invisible monsters.

"Tang Ka, Zhou Xiuyun, Tiantian!"

Chu Zhiyun considered herself to be the strongest of the team. She knew Tang Ka very well. Zhou Xiuyun was a weak civilian, and it was needless to mention Tiantian, who was just a baby.

If she were the different person as she had been, she would've only called Tang Ka and wouldn't have cared about Zhou Xiuyun and Tiantian at all.

After all, they were strangers whom she had never met before.

But for some reason, she remembered the fun Tiantian had in the playground and heard her giggles again. No matter how hard Chu Zhiyun shook her head, she couldn't get Tiantian out of her head.

"Is this what the extraterrestrial devils do?"

The big girl thought to herself, Is this what the extraterrestrial devils do? Damn it! Damn it!

While cursing, she struggled in the water hoping to find out where Tiantian was.

However, the currents here were too chaotic. Since almost eight sewers congregated here, they generated a handful of enormous swirls that were mixed with fallen leaves and garbage. She could barely keep her eyes open in the water, let alone seeing anyone in it.

Her voice was simply echoing in the dark sewer. There was no telling Tang Ka or anyone else could hear her.

There was no telling if it were her illusion, but she heard the cries of a little girl. She was about to swim to the sound when something grabbed her ankle and pulled her to the bottom of the water!

Caught unprepared, Chu Zhiyun had a few mouthfuls of filthy water and was almost suffocated.

When she surfaced again, the light was already gone, and she had been blown to the depths of one of the sewers.

The water currents were so powerful that her chest seemed to be smitten by an invisible hammer.

"How can the water in a sewer be so powerful?"

Chu Zhiyun found it hard to believe. "It doesn't make any sense!"

Whether or not it made sense, she couldn't do anything except floating alongside the current as she was almost passing out.

Her strength faded away from her limbs and her fingers, and her head was turning fuzzy. Gradually, she sank to the bottom of this swamp.

Is this… the punishment of the gods?

Chu Zhiyun thought with her last consciousness. I was wrong. I betrayed the gods under the seduction of the extraterrestrial devils. So, I lost the favor of the gods, and I have to die in dark, filthy water?"

Right then…

She suddenly sensed that someone hugged her with strong arms and took her away from the dark abyss and the shadow of death, pulling her from the stinky filthy water to the shore!

Crack!

The person broke a glow stick and waved it, driving the darkness away with its light.

Under the halo, the person seemed to be an envoy of the gods.

Chu Zhiyun observed it carefully, and when her eyes were finally accustomed to the light, she discovered that it was Tang Ka who saved her!

The young man couldn't have looked more resolute and determined, and he was holding a familiar blanket in his arms. Inside the blanket was exactly Tiantian who was moving like a kitten!

"Wow…"

Feeling that she had just been softened, Chu Zhiyun couldn't help but moan.

Chapter 2759: Domineering Young Man!

"Monitor, are you alright?"

Tang Ka was briefly stunned. He moved his ears to Chu Zhiyun's chest and listened for a while. "Have you drunk too much water? Why are you making this weird sound?"

"I… I'm fine!"

Chu Zhiyun didn't know why her face was so red and hot. She glared at Tang Ka and bit her lips. Then she asked curiously, "What happened just now? Why is Tiantian with you?"

"I… I have no idea either!"

Tang Ka said anxiously, "We seemed to have triggered a bomb, which blew up a hole on the ground. It happened to be a congregation point of sewers. After I fell into the water, I heard the cries of a baby in my dizziness, so I swam over and picked up Tiantian. Later, the dirty water got more and more powerful, and I couldn't control myself at all and could only float in the water until I arrived at this place. Then, I saw you struggling in the water, so I dived down and picked you up…"

"Okay…"

Chu Zhiyun thought for a moment and asked, "What about Tiantian's mother?"

"That I do not know."

Tang Ka shook his head and said, "I only picked up Tiantian, but I didn't see Zhou Xiuyun. She must've been separated from us."

They looked at the two ends of the sewer.

They were in a very spacious sewer, but the place was absolutely dark. Under the light of the glow stick, it could be vaguely seen that there were countless branches ahead.

It was absolutely impossible to find an ordinary person who had been missing for half an hour in such a maze.

Chu Zhiyun sighed and said, "It seems that we have to find a way out on our own now."

After a brief rest, Tang Ka supported Chu Zhiyun back to her feet.

Fortunately, neither of them was injured except for the minor scratches on their skin. Even Tiantian was not hurt either. She was staring at the glow stick in Tang Ka's hand with her eyes wide open!

For some reason, Chu Zhiyun grimaced at the little kid, and the little kid grimaced back at her. Both of them laughed.

But after the laugh, they had to face the reality.

Unfortunately, they lost all their assets in the turbulence. Tang Ka had even "accidentally" lost Zhou Xiuyun's map.

They had abandoned their portable crystal processors, which could locate them, at the very beginning too, or they would've been found out by the purge troop.

In other words, they had completely lost their way.

What had happened had happened, and there was no point to lament. They simply held each other's hands and resisted the coldness with each other's warmth, before they pressed forward in the sewer.

There was no telling if it was propitious or not, but they saw a well linked to the ground, as well as a ladder that wasn't completely rusted.

Further ahead, there was a pit dozens of meters deep, leading to places darker and more unknown.

Looking at each other, Tang Ka tied Tiantian to his back with a belt before he climbed the ladder.

He climbed a dozen meters and met a rusty cover. However, for two elites who had received the harshest training since childhood, it was not a problem.

Tang Ka laid his hand on the cover and vibrated his palm. Soon, the rust fell, and the cover was lifted without a sound.

The two of them crawled out of the manhole and breathed in the fresh air, feeling that they had been reborn.

Narrowing her eyes, Chu Zhiyun observed the signs on the buildings, only to discover that they had ended up in the southern suburb of New Joy City.

She did not expect that she would be carried so far away without bumping into anything on the way or being stuck. The journey turned out to be not as dangerous as it appeared.

However, as a result, they were now very far away from the shelter that Zhou Xiuyun mentioned at the beginning.

Now, the Holy Light Academy and the purge troop must have learned that they went missing. Chances were that the whole city was crowded with hunters. It was impossible for them to pass through the downtown area.

While Chu Zhiyun was pondering, Tang Ka suddenly said delightedly, "Monitor, check this out!"

Chu Zhiyun looked at where Tang Ka was pointing at, only to see a fairly large body shop that was half civil and half military. Both the civil shuttles and the military shuttles could be repaired here too.

"We're so lucky that we were carried to the bottom of a body shop. There must be a well-function shuttle with full fuel ready for us to use!"

Tang Ka winked at Chu Zhiyun. "Monitor, is this not a favor from the gods?"

Chu Zhiyun failed to come up with anything, but she felt weird. Between her and Tang Ka, she was the commander, and Tang Ka was supposed to be her subordinate. Even this escape had been initiated by her, and Tang Ka only joined it halfway.

However, why did Tang Ka become so active and "domineering" all of a sudden?

She didn't really dislike it, but she was quite unused to it. She wondered why Tang Ka had changed even more greatly than she did.

While Chu Zhiyun was still thinking, she found that Tang Ka had already run into the body shop excitedly.

"Hey, wait for me, Tang Ka!"

Chu Zhiyun hurried to go after him, wondering why she seemed to have turned into Tang Ka's sidekick.

The body shop was exactly like Tang Ka had speculated. Inside an enormous workshop were dozens of shuttles that were being maintained.

When Chu Zhiyun came in, Tang Ka had already scanned all the shuttles and chosen a silver streamlined shuttle. He was pushing a trolley of components and raising the maintenance scaffold.

"Monitor, this is the one!"

Tang Ka said excitedly, "We're quite lucky. This is a light high-speed shuttle for reconnaissance from the Imperium. It's fast and boasts a rather powerful spiritual shield. Also, its maintenance is almost done. It can be activated as soon as we put fuel in!"

"Oh? Really?"

Chu Zhiyun didn't know why Tang Ka was so familiar with the military shuttles of the Imperium. She thought for a moment and asked, "What are you doing if its maintenance has been completed?"

"I'm attempting a minor modification to see if I can completely block its noises and spiritual ripples so that we can escape without alarming anyone."

Tang Ka licked his lips and said, "Rest assured. I got this. It will be done in a minute!"

Chu Zhiyun was at a loss again.

Why was her heart racing?

Was this how the invasion of the extraterrestrial devils feel?

She felt that her wisdom had all been flushed away in the dirty water just now, and her head went completely blank as Tang Ka pushed Tiantian into her arms. She watched Tang Ka do his work in dizziness.

"Why are his hands so fast and… perfect now?"

Chu Zhiyun thought, Also, aren't our maintenance and recognition courses for civil magic equipment? Why is he capable of modifying such a piece of complicated military magic equipment like an armed shuttle?

Chu Zhiyun knew that maintenance was different from modification. Maintenance was about restoring a piece of magic equipment to its original look, whereas modification was about improving the performance with whatever materials you had. The latter was much more challenging!

Tang Ka was a teenager anyway. However capable he was, he couldn't have been able to maintain a shuttle of the Covenant Alliance, let alone one from the Imperium. However…

"It's done, monitor."

Tang Ka patted off the oil on his hand and made a "please" gesture. "Come on in!"

"What?"

Chu Zhiyun was stunned again.

"Come in, and let's get out of here!"

Tang Ka frowned. "Monitor, you seem to have turned a lot slower. Why?"

"Who… Who's slow?"

Chu Zhiyun glared at Tang Ka and climbed into the shuttle with Tiantian.

Since it was a high-speed shuttle for reconnaissance, its cabin was naturally not very big. She was almost stuck to Tang Ka's back and could smell his hot hands.

"You're going to fly it?"

Chu Zhiyun looked at the complicated operation menu. "It's very difficult to fly a shuttle. If you make any mistake, the shuttle might spin at seven hundred kilometers an hour. Are you sure you can do this?"

"I'll have a try!"

Tang Ka smiled. "Haven't we all been implanted with information on flying a shuttle?"

"That can't be enough."

Chu Zhiyun said, "Why don't you let me fly it?"

"Monitor, please trust me. Every distinguished commander should trust their most excellent soldier."

Tang Ka's hands turned into shadows as they rolled on the operation menu. Infinite glittering runes and numbers appeared and shined in his hands.

Hum!

The high-speed shuttle floated after a tiny noise.

Weirdly, although the output of the engine had been boosted to 120%, and even the shell of the shuttle was shaking, the shuttle was not making any sound except the hum at the beginning. It was as quiet as a cloud!

Chapter 2760: Game Over!

"Wow…"

Chu Zhiyun made a weird sound for the second time, and she stared at the back of Tang Ka's head in disbelief.

"Have you buckled up, monitor?"

Tang Ka's hands dashed among runes and numbers quickly like two bolts of lightning. As he operated, the maximal output of the shuttle was increasing: 130%… 150%… 180%!

In other words, after Tang Ka's five-minute modification, the high-speed shuttle, which had been as fast as lightning in the first place, was almost twice as fast as previously!

How… How was it possible?

It was not just about the circuits of spiritual energy and the magic equipment structure. But more importantly, a 180% speed would bring too much burden for beginners like Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun to handle.

Most likely, they would hit the wall the moment the shuttle was activated.

However, there was nothing Chu Zhiyun could do at this point. Besides, intimidated by the air around Tang Ka, she could not argue with him at all. She could only hold Tiantian in her arms and examined the safety measures once again. She then nodded and said, "I'm ready!"

"In that case, let's go!"

Tang Ka snapped his fingers, and green buffer gels spurted out and enshrouded the three of them, but Tang Ka's head was exposed and clasped by a silver neural interaction helmet.
