The light on the two sides of the helmet flashed quickly. The shuttle seemed absolutely still, but the world around was falling back quickly into narrow, long lines. It took Chu Zhiyun quite a long time before she realized that they were already flying!

"Well…"

Chu Zhiyun bulged her eyes as she looked at the world outside, which had almost turned into two gray walls.

She realized that Tang Ka was not bragging but had truly tamed this high-speed shuttle, which was flying at the maximal speed and the lowest attitude. It was almost only one foot away from the ground.

In such a way, the shuttle could be covered by the skyscrapers as well as the radiations from the ground. Even if the purge troop was scanning them on a large scale, it would be much less likely for them to be discovered.

Also, the shuttle was not making any sound as it was rushing, and the spiritual shield of this shuttle had a gray layer of camouflage thanks to Tang Ka's modification. At first look, it was only a ripple in the air that was flashing!

However, it was not hard to imagine how dangerous and difficult this was.

The closer the shuttle was to the ground, the more obstacles there would be. The buildings, the walls, the debris of crystal tanks, and even landmines that could jump could all be fatal.

The shuttle was already flying over five hundred kilometers per hour.

At such a speed, even a blunt fragment would be enough to penetrate through the engine unit of the shuttle and kill them!

Chu Zhiyun was one of the most excellent students in the Holy Light Academy, and her sight and reaction were among the best of her peers. However, it was still hard for her to recognize the objects coming at her at five hundred kilometers per hour. The skyscrapers were cutting at her like black sabers, and she almost couldn't stop herself from crying out aloud.

Tang Ka, however, was absolutely unhurried, as if he were simply strolling in the city. He took turns right next to the glass windows of the skyscrapers multiple times without breaking any of them.

When they encountered a building that had collapsed from the middle, instead of flying above the ruins, Tang Ka chose to swoop into the gaps of the ruins and flew right through the remains of the building!

Chu Zhiyun felt that her heart was jumping out of her throat.

Tang Ka, however, yawned and smacked his lips in boredom.

"T-Tang Ka, what's wrong?" asked Chu Zhiyun with a shivering voice.

"Huh? I'm fine. What 'what's wrong'?"

Tang Ka turned back and looked at his monitor. "Why do you ask?"

"Ah!"

Chu Zhiyun screamed, "Don't look back. Eyes on the road. Eyes on the road! We're about to hit it!"

Tang Ka stared at his monitor innocently and operated quickly again. Then, the shuttle slanted and passed between two plazas that were almost next to each other.

After that, Tang Ka yawned again so hard that he was almost shedding tears.

"You… You're yawning?"

Chu Zhiyun didn't know how she should describe her feelings.

"Yes. I didn't sleep much last night, and I'm feeling rather drowsy."

Tang Ka rubbed his eyes. "That's why I'm so slow. Don't scold me."

"That's why you're so… slow?"

Chu Zhiyun realized that she and Tang Ka probably differed greatly on the definition of "slow".

Also, she noticed a very special vibe from Tang Ka, whose eyes were half-closed; a vibe that was hard to describe but somehow made people want to beat him up.

"Besides, there's a baby in this shuttle. We should be more careful. Although this is boring, safety always comes first!"

While talking, Tang Ka groped below his seat for a moment and found a can of juice. He opened the can and took a huge mouthful of the juice, before he moaned in satisfaction and continued, "The engine is only being preheated. When we reach the suburbs that are more empty, we'll accelerate!"

Chu Zhiyun suddenly realized that, while they were changing courses frequently at five hundred kilometers per hour, Tiantian did not cry at all, and she did not feel any discomfort, such as loss of weight or overloading.

She lowered her head, only to see that Tiantian was observing the jelly-like gel around her as well as the rushing world outside!

Noticing that the big sister was looking at her, the baby giggled in delight, not bothered by the separation from her mother yet.

"When did you start drinking juice? Why do I not know it at all?"

Chu Zhiyun looked at Tang Ka's smile and found it weirder and weirder.

"There are a lot of things you don't know, but it's fine. I'll tell you all of them later, my beloved monitor!"

Tang Ka said, "Now, all you need to do is to trust me and let me take care of everything. It's going to be fine! I promise you that we will escape from this wretched city and our fate. We will find a shelter—a real shelter!

"Those idiots… No, those brainless scumbags must be searching for us in the north of the city, aren't they? Hehe, by the time they realize that something is not right, we'll be taking a hot bath in the shelter! Do you trust me, monitor?"

"Huh…"

Chu Zhiyun frowned and said, "I would love to trust you, but doesn't the glittering exclamation marks as well as the number behind it that is getting smaller and smaller indicate that dozens of flying swords are coming at us?"

Tang Ka was briefly stunned, and the cabin was immediately filled by earsplitting alarms.

The shining flying swords behind them were so close that they could be seen with the naked eye.

"How is it possible?"

Tang Ka's pupils were constricted to the minimum. He was so furious as if someone had just slapped him in the face.

When he raised his hand speed, Chu Zhiyun finally understood why Tang Ka felt bored earlier. She felt that she was not inside a high-speed shuttle, but on a shooting star that was breaking the limits of the three-dimensional universe!

Crack! Crack! Crack! Crack!

The shuttle was accelerated to seven hundred kilometers per hour and dodged 37 times within one second before it decelerated to 350 kilometers per hour. It finally got rid of the pursuing flying swords when it almost fell apart in midair.

Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom!

Those flying swords exploded around the shuttle, raising a torrent of hot waves and shards that submerged the shuttle again and again.

The shuttle had only light armor in the first place. Despite Tang Ka's modifications, it could not take the attacks of so many flying swords without being damaged.

The tempered glass was cracking, and the alloy shell was dented and even torn apart by the shards.

At a supersonic speed, even a tiny hole invisible to the naked eye could lead to a disaster. Tang Ka had to lower the speed while he cursed.

By the time he saw the pursuers behind him clearly, however, he had lost the strength to curse.

From every direction of New Joy City, in the middle of the plazas and skyscrapers, thousands of crystal suits and shuttles were rising. They were like glittering locusts in the cold mist.

All of them aimed their lamps at Tang Ka's shuttle, suggesting that the previous barrage of flying swords was not coincidental and that the enemy did know the shuttle's coordinates.

Behind those buildings, the floating fortress that was as huge as a mountain also moved close in deafening noises to Tang Ka's and Chu Zhiyun's faces like a cloud that was on fire.

Chapter 2761: Break A Fortress From Within!

On the floating fortresses, thousands of cannons were unleashing the most aggressive waves of spiritual energy as if thousands of ferocious beasts were opening their bloody mouths at the same time. Even though they were thousands of meters away, Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun could both hear the hisses in the air. There was no telling whether it was dust being shattered by electric arcs or if it was the howling of hungry animals.

The shuttles and Exos were also gathered into two swarms of locusts that came at Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun from left and right.

Before the shuttles and the Exos arrived, the second wave of flying swords had smashed at the enemy in an impenetrable web.

Tang Ka cursed, and his hand speed increased yet again as he operated the shuttle. The shuttle did not seem to be accelerated, but it quickly dodged within limited range leaving overlapping shadows behind it while keeping the previous speed.

A hundred flying swords passed through the shadows and exploded up ahead. The blast of the explosion, as well as the direction, angle, and power of the blast, had already been calculated by Tang Ka, who used it as a propeller to further boost the shuttle on their way of escape.

"Ahhhhhhhhhhh!"

Tang Ka's eyes were on fire, and his fingers left blood on the control panel.

"Tang Ka…"

Chu Zhiyun did not know what to say as she looked.

Boom!

Right when they broke the second wave of flying swords, the main cannon of the floating fortress was fully charged and roared.

A stream of energy as hot as magma darted through the sky unhurriedly and did not explode until it reached Tang Ka's head. The rain of fire covered three square kilometers nearby.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The area immediately turned into an ocean of fire, and the blast savaged the sky like an invisible army.

This time, it didn't help no matter what a brilliant pilot Tang Ka was.

It was an impossible mission to fight a whole army and such an enormous floating fortress with only one shuttle.

The blast that the enemy released could easily blow up all the skyscrapers within three kilometers. How could this shuttle avoid this torrent of destruction?

The enemy was only showing them mercy by not firing at them directly.

If the cannon had locked onto them, Tang Ka would've been killed even if he were ten times more skilled at flying a shuttle.

Beep! Beep! Beep! Beep!

The shell of the shuttle was completely deformed, and most of its armor had been knocked away by the blast, revealing the broken framework down below. The balance rune arrays and the anti-gravity rune arrays were wasted too. The shuttle was beyond control and spiraled all the way to the ground.

Tang Ka gritted his teeth and did his best, but he could only make sure that the shuttle hit the ground with its bottom so that the passengers on it wouldn't be hurt.

The shuttle itself, however, was reduced to garbage and couldn't fly again.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

When Tang Ka pulled Chu Zhiyun, who was holding Tiantian in her arms, out of the remains of the shuttle, they heard boots from the fire and smoke. Then, they saw moving coffins in front of them. The shining emblems on their chests suggested that they were the Purgers that Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun used to dream to be!

"Why?"

Chu Zhiyun felt like crying, but a fury was arising in her heart like poisoned wine, replacing her fear and devastation.

She stared at the expressionless Purgers with bloodshot eyes.

"Why?"

Tang Ka was even more dumbfounded, unable to believe his eyes. After a long stun, he roared in his head, "Grandpa Yao, what's going on? Didn't you say that everything was under your control, and your plan had never gone wrong before? Didn't you say that nothing could possibly go wrong? Why have we been caught? They even moved the floating fortress to capture us. How did they know that we were here? Grandpa Yao? Grandpa Yao!"

Tang Ka shouted for a long time but received no response. Grandpa Yao, who had been attached to him and who had been enhancing his sight, his reaction, and his computation ability until just now, had completely disappeared and couldn't be sensed at all.

An idea suddenly popped up in Tang Ka's head. Damn it. Has this "Grandpa Yao" escaped seeing that he can't resolve the trouble he caused?

"Grandpa Yao? Grandpa Yao!"

Tang Ka was covered in a cold sweat. "You can't go away just like this! What can we do without you, Grandpa Yao?"

"Why are you so anxious? I'm right here."

When the young man was about to burst into tears, Li Yao finally responded casually in his head.

"Grandpa Yao, where were you? I was so scared just now!"

Tang Ka almost shed tears.

"I'm sorry, but I just had an arduous battle with my mental devil. My mental devil was so shameless that it urged me to disconnect and escape!" Li Yao explained.

"W-What?"

Tang Ka was confused. "Are you not a mental devil? Then what's your mental devil? A mental devil's mental devil?"

"Well, it's very complicated. Later, well, maybe soon enough, you will see what my mental devil is exactly."

Li Yao said, "All in all, I solemnly scolded it and made it realize its mistakes in shame. Then, I analyzed why things were looking good and everything was under control. This game is not over yet, and we can still play it!"

"Everything is under control?"

Dumbfounded, Tang Ka watched the Exos approaching them from the ground and the shuttles blocking them in the sky. The enormous floating fortress, in particular, pressed his heart like a stone. He could not understand why Li Yao was shameless enough to say that "everything is under control".

"Things are getting more and more interesting. To deal with two Mr. Nobodies, the Holy Light Academy and the purge troop deployed so many soldiers and even the floating fortress. Why?"

Li Yao said casually, "Have my identity been exposed? Unlikely. If my identity were exposed, those Ultimate Benevolence Masters would've sent a hundred times more soldiers to hunt us down—not that they could really change anything—as long as they're smarter than preschoolers."

"Grandpa Yao!"

Tang Ka almost really cried. "We're in the middle of an emergency. Would you please stop bragging about yourself?"

"Those who are not familiar with me often think I'm bragging, but those who are familiar with me know what a modest person I am."

Li Yao said, "I'm only speaking the truth and analyzing the current incident.

"For all we know, the enemy does not know my existence, so their target is either you or your monitor. If your identities are fine, something must be greatly wrong with your parents, or your 'gene providers'.

"You're both the Children of Holy Light. Hehe. I believe that the secrets behind this seemingly sacred and glorious name will be revealed soon, and I'll be able to find someone of a higher level. Chances are that I can even swing to the Ultimate Benevolence Masters in one step.

"This must be what they call a silver lining, isn't it?

"As for you, just rest assured. The current situation is just a minor accident. Since I promised that I would protect you, you will definitely reach the shelter safely. Hehe. You're under my protection. Nobody can hurt you in front of me. Even though the stars in all the universe come at you as meteorites, I will blow them up one by one!"

"Then what are we waiting for?"

Tang Ka said in delight, "If you're so awesome, Grandpa Yao, please unleash your full might through my body. You can eliminate this purge troop first, shoot down the armed shuttles in the sky, and then blow up the floating fortress!"

Li Yao: "…"

"Don't worry about my body. I can take it!"

Tang Ka gritted his teeth and said, "Bring it on, Grandpa Yao. Unleash your infinite might. I'm sure it's going to be an eye-opener for me. Come on!"

"Come on?"

Li Yao gnashed his teeth angrily, "What I said was rhetorical. Rhetorical! Do you understand? This enormous floating fortress has thousands of cannons on it. It can easily suppress a city on its way. Look at its spiritual shield. Even the air around it is being warped. Chances are that it can resist the bombardment of the main guns of a starship dozens of times! You're asking me to fight this iron fortress bare-handedly with your body? Are you sure you're not kidding?

"Really, just use your brain. Have you never heard the saying that 'the hardest fortresses are always broken from within'? Do you see those armed shuttles and Exos? They're flying into the floating fortress with captives. Look, aren't those captives your classmates, the other 'Children of Holy Light' who are involved in this ultimate test?

"If my guess is correct, it's where you and your monitor will be captured and sent to too. The floating fortress is not a common attack platform but the base of the whole scheme.. We can turn the floating fortress upside down from inside after we figure out what has been going on. Wouldn't it be much easier than fighting the fortress the hard way from the outside?"

Chapter 2762: Brain Experiment

Tang Ka wanted to say something, but Li Yao had already crawled to the deepest part of his soul and seemed to have melded with his brain. There was no sound coming from him anymore.

Tang Ka raised his head and found that more than ten soldiers of the sanitation unit had surrounded them. The black barrels of their guns were right in front of them and aimed at their heads.

Tang Ka could feel that his fingertips were cramping, All his cells were being burnt to the point that they were about to explode. His skin could even sense the flow of a breeze hundreds of meters away. Dozens of battle plans were popping up in his brain at any moment. It was not until then that he

realized that ‘Old Yao’ had not abandoned him but was fully prepared for the battle. He was somewhat relieved.

Dozens of armed shuttles flew above his head. The interweaving mystic rays illuminated both him and the squad leader. He felt that his every vein and every nerve was being scanned by the enemy.

But ‘Old Yao’ was like a vague mist that flowed to the deepest part of his body where he did not know what was going on. He was not detected at all.

Beeps were echoing inside their helmets, indicating that everything was normal. Very soon, more than ten cold ‘coffins’ continued moving toward them.

Chu Zhiyun roared and rushed toward the ‘coffin’ without bothering about anything else.

She was truly the most distinguished student of the Holy Light Institute in recent years. Without a crystal suit, she had knocked over three purifiers in crystal suits and snatched their weapons with barely any weapons.

However, the weapon of the purifier had an exclusive fingerprint and blood identification system that could only be activated by the real owner. The moment it fell into somebody else’s hands, intense electric arcs were immediately released, which made Chu Zhiyun twitch violently. His hair was all

standing up, and black blood was flowing out of his eyes, ears, and nostrils!

Chu Zhiyun seemed to be locked in a cage of electric arcs, but she did not give up her hope for freedom. Gritting her teeth, she staggered forward three steps. At this moment, her body seemed to have overlapped with Liao Meng’s in the morning, and the outcome was the same. She fell to the ground, her

limbs still cramping.

“Class monitor…”

Tang Ka felt a lump in his throat and could not help but shed hot tears.

He felt that every muscle on his body had been tightened, as if he were a screw that had been twisted all the way to the end. He had an urge to fight a random cleaner.

“Don’t be hasty. Let’s see how it goes.”

Li Yao’s high-pitched voice came from the depths of the young man’s soul. “It seems that their target is your class monitor. They won’t keep a close eye on a nobody like you. This is our chance—our chance to figure everything out!”

As it tumed out, the purifiers had tied up Chu Zhiyun and installed shackles made of super alloys on almost every part of his body that could be shackled. The shackles were also carved with lightning streaks. The electromagnetic attraction between the shackles ensured that Chu Zhiyun’s hands and feet

were locked together.

As for Tang Ka, they simply put a pair of handcuffs on his back and did not restrict him from walking.

It seemed that the purifiers did not care about the thirteen-year-old boy at all. He was just a ‘bonus’ for their hunt for Chu Zhiyun.

Tang Ka’s behavior was in line with his timidness and obedience. He was so scared that he forgot to resist and simply shed tears. “What is going on? What is going on? I don’t know anything, Let me go. II don’t know anything!”

The purifiers whose faces were hidden behind the iron masks naturally turned a deaf ear to his struggles and screams. They escorted him and the squad leader to a flying shuttle.

But Little Tian Tian had been taken away by them on a different shuttle.

Tang Ka’s eyes were about to explode, but Li Yao told him to calm down. Little Tian Tian was just a prop for the enemy. She should be fine for now. By then, Li Yao would have a way to rescue her.

Just like that, the two ‘traitors’ were captured and brought to the floating fortress by the shuttle.

While he was still in midair, Tang Ka craned his head to the window and looked down, only to discover that intense fire and smoke were popping up everywhere in the downtown area. There were also signs of escape and resistance. Very soon, hundreds of flying shuttles took off and flew to the floating

fortress like them.

It seemed that ‘Master Yao’ was right. The ultimate test was over. All the Children of Holy Light would be tied up and taken to the floating fortress without exception.

“What… are we exactly?”

Tang Ka thought silently.

The closer he got to the floating fortress, the more he could see the magnificence and vastness of the iron city. It was like a man-made celestial body that had swallowed the sky. Even the entrance of the hangar was like the bloody mouth of a prehistoric beast that had swallowed dozens of shuttles.

Like two dusts that were blown away by the wind, Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun, together with dozens of other ignorant students, passed through the narrow and deep pathway escorted by a team of cleaners.

Very soon, the cabin door in front of them was opened, revealing a space as spacious as a palace or a valley. They were walking in the transparent tubes in the air, as if they were walking on a bridge between two valleys. The ‘crags’ on the two sides of the bridge were embedded with transparent cells that

looked like honeycombs!

Inside every transparent prison cell was a prisoner, or rather, a lunatic. When they observed carefully, they discovered that these people were all their classmates.

Not just the students who had been eliminated in the “Ultimate Test’, even the students who had been unable to keep up with the progress or had lost control of their emotions during the past few years of cultivation in the Holy Light Academy had been locked up in this place!

Some of the students had wooden expressions as they sat upright on their metal beds. Their eyes were hollow and unfocused as they stared in confusion at the nonexistent light spots in the air. They were like fossils that had been dried for millions of years.

Some of the students were pacing back and forth anxiously. Now and then, they would raise their fists and ruthlessly smash the transparent wall in front of them, leaving streaks of blood on the wall. Only after smashing their hands into a bloody mess did they sit down in satisfaction. Their mouths were

still muttering non-stop, making them look like animals wearing human skin.

Some of the students were wearing an enormous helmet on their head. The helmet was covered in colorful indicator lights and crystal wires that led to the walls. The helmet seemed to be connected to extremely powerful electric currents and spiritual waves, bringing them indescribable pain and making

them scream in pain that was worse than death. However, their hands and feet were locked, and some of their joints were even broken. They lost the ability to move and even lost the ability to end their lives.

Some of the students were locked in narrow and stuffy sleep capsules. Their bodies were soaked in bright green or pink thick liquids. They seemed to be caught in some kind of nightmare or illusion. Sometimes they were twitching, sometimes they were struggling, and sometimes they were chuckling.

There was no telling what they had seen or experienced in the dream.

‘What was most creepy was the dozens of cells at the bottom of the cliff.

There was no one inside the cells, but they were full of precise equipment and tubes that were as complicated as a maze. All kinds of suspicious liquids were flowing inside the tubes and eventually congregated into cylindrical glass tanks.

They had thought that there was nothing inside the glass tanks. But when they squinted and observed carefully, they discovered that a brain was bobbing up and down inside every glass tank.

Some of the brains even had spinal cord trailing behind them. They looked quite intact and fresh.

The other brains, on the other hand, had been stripped of a lot of parts, except for the central part, which looked like a rotten, deformed apple and seemed to have been there for many years.

All the brains, without exception, had been inserted into thousands of crystal wires, which gave them different stimuli. Under the stimuli, their brains would twitch slightly in response.

These brains were all alive!

Or rather, the owners of the brains were still alive on a certain level!

“What—what is the meaning of this?”

“Will we one day be like these students who, after going through all kinds of cruel experiments, are deprived of their bodies and only have their brains left, only to be thrown into the apparatus for research? And at this time… will we still be alive?”

“We are the Children of Holy Light, the most loyal believers of the gods and the greatest master. Glory and holiness flow in our veins. Why are you treating us like this?”

Right now, all the indifferent disguises of the Children of Holy Light had collapsed. They were venting their fear and desperation unscrupulously. Some of them were even sobbing.

Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun, on the other hand, were observing the scenes inside the cells on the two sides with their big, black eyes, hoping to gather more useful information.

They finally passed through the terrifying transparent tube and entered a room whose floor, walls, and ceiling were ivory and emitting sacred brilliance.

The seemingly gentle pure white brightness was actually like a poison that could corrupt the heart of every ‘Child of Holy Light. It penetrated into the skin and pores of every ‘Child of Holy Light’, corrupting their soul and nerves, and gave them a weird feeling that their brain had been penetrated through

and they could see everything clearly.

The Children of Holy Light looked at each other in bewilderment, shivering, and crying.

Some of them were gritting their teeth, with grunts echoing deep inside their nose and throat. They were trying to fight back, but there was nothing they could do.

Shua! Shua! Shua!

In front of the panicked teenagers, the ivory brilliance condensed into three hazy figures.

Although the three of them were of different genders, there was a fake smile that seemed to be carved out of the same mold on their faces, making it impossible to distinguish them. One could only tell their identity by the color of their uniform.

The one wearing the white uniform was the dean of the Holy Light Academy, Xia Yuren.

The one wearing a black uniform with a blood-stained battle emblem on his chest was ‘Qiu Yuanjia’, the commander of the purification troop.

The middle-aged woman standing in the middle of the two, who seemed to be in the highest position and was wearing a gray uniform with an unknown identity, was surprisingly Little Tian Tian’s mother, Zhou Xiuyun!

Chapter 2763: “Devil’S Child” Project

Pa! Pa! Pa!

Faced with the bewildered and frightened teenagers of Class 1, the three shadows clapped their hands with fake smiles at first, before they tapped their forehead, lips, and chest with their hands to show the highest courtesy of the Holy Alliance. Xia Yuren, dean of the Holy Light Institute, said softly, “Congratulations, students. You’ve just completed a marvelous test and provided a lot of valuable data for our research. You’ve made great contributions to the most sacred cause in the universe.

“I know that you are very confused and scared right now. There is no need for you to be confused or scared. Believe me. Whatever appears in front of you must be the guidance of the gods. Whatever is imposed on you must be the blessing of the gods. You just need to be calm and accept it.

“Ah. Today, two distinguished guests from afar were asked by the Great Sage to come here and celebrate your grand graduation ceremony’. It is not hard to tell how much the Great Sage values you, the Children of Holy Light’. This is Lieutenant General Qiu Yuanjia, commander of the ‘Ninth Purifying Troop’. She is a loyal and keen senior Purifier. As for this… Let’s call her ‘Ms. Zhou Xiuyun’. Her name is just a code name. The most important thing is the gift that the gods bestowed upon her. She is one of the best Meditation Healers and brain experts in the entire Covenant Alliance. She is also the supervisor of the ‘Devil’s Child Project’. In the next few years, she will replace me as your guardian. I believe that you will get along very well.”

“Hello, everyone.”

In the morning, in front of Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun, ‘Zhou Xiuyun’ was still unkempt and anxious. But right now, she was a completely different person. She seemed to be enshrouded in a faint mist. Although she was greeting the students with a smile, she was looking at the young men as if they were lab rats.

All the teenagers took a step back in fear because of her cold and merciless eyes.

Chu Zhiyun was in disbelief, too. It was not until their eyes met that she realized what was going on.

What is going on?

In a trance, Chu Zhiyun mumbled, “The so-called ‘ultimate test’ is simply a lie. No. Not just the ‘ultimate test’. The entire Holy Light Academy, including those who want to train us, the Children of Holy Light, into ‘purifiers’, are all fakes! From the very beginning, we were never selected and trained for the purpose of purifiers’. Lies. Everything is a lie. The entire Holy Light Academy is a lie!”

“It’s true that you boast keen insight and thorough thinking ability, just like your mother. That’s why we picked you and you.

‘Zhou Xiuyun’, the supervisor of the ‘Devil Child Project’, said calmly, “It’s true that you are not fostered to purify the troops. The selection criteria of the ordinary purifiers are not very high. Those who are slightly stronger than the ‘soldier bees’ and the ‘worker bees’ can fill the vacancy. When necessary, the cold spiritual puppets can complete the simple purification tasks. It is not worth building a ‘School of Holy Light’ and investing a lot of precious resources to nurture you.

“No, you are a hundred times more important than the ‘Purifier’. Don’t let your imagination run wild and think too lowly of yourselves. There is a reason why you entered the ‘School of Holy Light’. You all carry extremely special genes and are one-in-ten-thousand abnormal people’. You have a mission that is a hundred times more difficult and sacred than the ‘Purifier’. And you have ‘luckyly not let down’ and completed your mission very well without you knowing it. Especially in the ‘Ultimate Test’, many people released their natural instincts to their heart’s content and displayed the deepest secrets of their genes. Liao Meng, Chu Zhiyun, and even the once inconspicuous Tang Ka. Uncontrollable and extremely powerful strength and will burst forth from the depths of your hearts. It’s truly amazing. You can’t help but want to praise the gods for creating the perfection of our kind of life.”

While talking, Zhou Xiuyun made a gesture of praise the gods’.

Chu Zhiyun, on the other hand, was shivering. There was no telling whether she was angry or scared. She asked in a shivering voice, “If we are not purifiers, what are we exactly?”

“You are the Children of Holy Light.

Zhou Xiuyun replied casually, “Or rather, ‘Devil’s Child’.”

“Devil’s Child?”

Chu Zhiyun ground his teeth and chewed on the title with his molars. “What is a ‘devil child’?”

Zhou Xiuyun didn’t answer him directly. Instead, she opened her arms, and ivory brilliance of holiness and brilliance flowed out of her body, as if she had turned into pure white, fluffy wings on her back. Even her voice sounded more airy and transparent. She said from the bottom of her heart. “Praise be to the gods for creating all things in the universe and us. Even before we fell into eternal rest, they had already planned our fate and bestowed upon us the special and sacred abilities of the five great masters. We can read the prophecy and miracles left by the gods.

and destroy the world.

universe.

Zhou Xiuyun’s tone was sharper and sharper. When she said ‘heresy’, her teeth seemed to have turned into two

sharp guillotines that were going to cut every word into pieces.

“Doubt the existence of the gods!”

All the Children of Holy Light gasped, including Chu Zhiyun and Tang Ka.

Even the most rebellious Chu Zhiyun did not dare to imagine a world without gods.

The ‘heretics’ were truly insane!

“The ‘heresy’ is the most fatal threat for the entire Covenant Alliance. In many cases, it is even more dangerous than our external enemies, the Imperium of True Human Beings.

Zhou Xiuyun explained, “The ‘heretics’ are not like the ordinary ‘contaminators’ of the extraterrestrial devils. They may not show strong emotions and desires, nor will they be driven by the low-level emotions and lust. Instead, they focus their attention on the things of a higher level. They will even disguise their feelings and thoughts and pretend to be the most loyal believers and warriors of the gods so that they can crawl into the leadership of the entire Covenant Alliance. Our central nerves and our ‘brain’ will trick more people into believing their wrong thoughts and gradually expand the range of their pollution. The innocent and innocent citizens of the Covenant Alliance will be greatly confused.

“Take the war in the Black Wind Defense Line, one of the most important defense lines of the Imperium of True Human Beings, for example.

“At that time, the entire defense line of the Imperium of True Human Beings collapsed, and tens of thousands of starships were in chaos. It was a great opportunity for the fleet of the Covenant Alliance to crush the imperium and restore the glory of the gods to the entire universe.

“But because of the appearance of an extremely dangerous ‘heresy’ in our organization, countless people were persuaded to join her evil organization in order to compete with the five great sages. The great sages were forced to devote a lot of precious resources, soldiers, and computational ability to internal cleansing, which eventually led to the failure of the frontline. In the end, the imperium managed to secure the second defense line, which was a tug-of-war that lasted a hundred years.

“Ah…”

The young men had studied the entire process of the Battle of Black Wind a hundred years ago in the ‘School of Holy Light’. They did not know that there was such an internal reason for the retreat of the Holy Alliance.

Even Li Yao was secretly surprised. He thought to himself that the fall of the Black Wind Sector a hundred years ago was indeed the starting point of everything. Countless turbulent events in the future, including the expedition of the Black Wind Fleet to the Star Glory Federation, the rise of ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu, the opportunity for the Infinite Commerce Alliance to control the economy and trade of the world outside the imperium, and the glamorous return of the Black Wind King, were all related to the war.

Right now, even the ‘heresy’ inside the Covenant Alliance had shown up!

There was no way that the ideology of human beings could be blocked or exterminated. As long as human beings had a highly developed logical thinking ability, it was impossible for them to stop the birth of all kinds of weird ideas. As far as Li Yao was concerned, it was impossible for the opinions of the five supreme masters to be completely unified. They all had their own ideas and plans. Therefore, it was not strange at all that a few Theretics’ were born in the internal strife of the leadership.

However, he could not figure out how the ‘heresy’ of the leadership was related to the so-called ‘devil children’..

Chapter 2764: : Heresy Child!

“The Holy Accord Alliance is different from the evil kingdoms on earth such as the Imperium of True Human Beings. We are connected in accordance with the sacred agreements of the primeval era. We are an inseparable, selfless community of destiny. We are a sacred, magnificent, eternal life form that

transcends everything, transcends everything, and rules everything.

Zhou Xiuyun’s eyes were almost transparent. She seemed to be able to enter another world through her eyes, a brand-new universe that was above the three-dimensional universe. Her voice was the opposite of calm and craziness at the same time, but it was also very persuasive. Even the young men in

prison were attracted to her and listened to her attentively.

“The gods are the souls and the will of the community of destiny; the sages are the brain cells and the central nerves of the community of destiny; the fleet commanders and the governors of the planets are the nerves of the community of destiny; the special professionals such as the purifiers and the

lurkers are the red and white cells of the community of destiny; the workers, farmers, and soldiers at the frontline are the blood, bones, and muscles of the enormous life form.

“Everybody has their own destiny. Only when all the parts are united and carrying out the sacred destiny faithfully can the enormous community of destiny control and protect the entire universe.

“But the ‘heresy’ invaded the blood, marrow, internal organs, nerves, braincells, and even the soul of the ‘universal community’ like a deadly virus, leading to the corruption of the body, the tearing of nerves, and even the mutation of braincells. It cast a gloomy shadow over our sacred alliance.

“Of course, the search and treatment for the ‘virus’ has never stopped. Over the hundreds of years, we have indeed identified thousands of ‘heretics’ and defended the health, harmony, and order of the community of destiny.

“However, it is useless to eliminate the ‘virus’ on the surface without analyzing the source and the mechanism of its formation. Every time we find a ‘virus’, it means that there are ten thousand other deeply-hidden viruses inside our body. It even means that certain people are naturally carrying some kind

of ‘genetic defect’, some kind of ‘genetic bomb’ that was embedded into our body by the extraterrestrial devils during the primeval war hundreds of thousands of years ago.

“If the most fundamental genetic defects and mutation features of the virus are not found, the ‘virus’ may come back at any time. In the most extreme cases, everybody will be deeply infected and set off a ‘heretical frenzy’.

“However, it is extremely complicated and dangerous to study the causes of ‘viruses’, or ‘heretics’.

“Because the ‘heretics’ are all adults who have been deeply possessed. Their souls have been soaked in the black venom for too long and have lost the possibility of being saved. They have become stubborn and cunning.

“They will think of all kinds of ways to hide their real thoughts and emotions and their genetic defects. They will even commit suicide the moment they are discovered by us and burn their brain and every cell into ashes with incendiary bombs, leaving nothing that we can study at all.

“Even the ‘heretics’ who are occasionally captured have a way to quietly destroy their own brains—they would rather die than cooperate with our research.

“Faced with such a tricky situation, most of the research can only be carried out from the side. Thankfully, the Covenant Alliance has a perfect record of the birth and genetic heritage of the population. No matter how the ‘heretics’ try to hide their feelings and their will, their birth certificates and blood

ties cannot be concealed.

“Let’s assume that the ‘heresy’ is caused by a certain genetic defect. Then, there must be secrets about the ‘heresy’ in the people who carry similar genes, namely their relatives and even their children. Therefore, if a ‘heresy’ is captured alive by us, we will extract his life seed and combine it with the life

seed of another ‘heresy’ to produce offspring with double genetic defects. If the ‘heresy’ burns his body and his brain, we will find the brothers and sisters closest to him by blood, or his parents, and extract the life seed to produce ‘brothers’ whose genes are similar to his.

They were devil children, carriers of genetic defects and fatal viruses, destined to be cursed, forsaken by the universe, and destroyed by the Covenant Alliance!

Some of the teenagers could not help but cry.

Some of the teenagers felt that their legs were shaking. They sat on the ground and scratched their hair hard, finding it hard to believe.

“There’s no need to panic, despair, and shame. Didn’t Dean Xia say just now? Everything that has befallen you is a gift from the gods?

“You are different from your genetic providers,” Zhou Xiuyun said casually. “You didn’t cause any substantial damage to the entire Covenant Alliance. Besides, it was not your own will that you ended up like this. Therefore, you can be saved, and you can even make great contributions to the Holy Accord Alliance.

That’s why I’m willing to tell you everything. I want you to be clear about your origins, your birthright, and your sacred mission. Together with our research, you will continue to make the contributions you deserve, wash away the sins of you and your genetic providers, and make up for everything that

they destroyed.

“What—what kind of contribution?”

Chu Zhiyun took a deep breath and tried to keep his voice calm. He gritted his teeth and asked, “What is our future exactly?”

“Your future.

The fake smile on Zhou Xiuyun’s face was replaced by coldness again. She seemed to find the word ‘future’ absurd. She said calmly, “It’s hard to say. It depends on the brain scan and subsequent tests.

“Maybe, if your brain is very interesting and of great research value, we will erase your memories from more than ten years ago and throw you into a brand-new experiment as an ordinary soldier or miner. and create more ‘surprises’.

Chapter 2765: Venomous Like A Scorpion!

Zhou Xiuyun opened her arms and embraced all the young men warmly.

If one ignored the content of her words and only looked at her kind expression and the milky white ‘holy light’ behind her back, it really seemed as if she was giving the teenagers a great ‘blessing’.

However, the ‘gospel’ made all the teenagers shudder in anger.

“It’s—it’s—it’s hilarious!”

Chu Zhiyun finally couldn’t hold it back any longer. He was so angry that two streams of tears were rolling down his face. He shouted, “What kind of ‘gift’ is this? Even the demon kings in the deepest level of hell would not be so sordid and evil! And you are calling us ‘heretics’? No. We just want to know the

true face of the Great Master and hear the truth of the gods in person. We didn’t expect that your true face would be like this!

The girl struggled with all her might. The shackles on her hands and feet were rattling. The streaks of lightning were shining in the blink of an eye. too.

way for the two of us to redeem our mother, who has been corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils and fallen into the deepest level of hell forever.

“You”

Chu Zhiyun’s mind was in a mess. The two rows of teeth that had just been frozen finally collided with each other with cracking sounds.

Chapter 2779: Omnipresent!

He soon discovered that he was in two very wonderful states.

In the first 'unfolding' state, his consciousness could appear randomly in a crystal processor, a piece of magical equipment, or a crystal camera within several square kilometers, where it could collect, absorb, and digest the tremendous information that came with it.

But such a state could only last for a moment. After a moment, his consciousness would 'collapse' and condense into a tiny dot where he could not see or hear anything. Except for the thousands of weird dreams that were still haunting him, he did not know whether or not he was still real.

The range of 'a few square kilometers' was based on the debris of the floating fortress, or rather, the remaining mainframe crystal processor of the floating fortress. It was very likely that his consciousness was still hiding inside the crystal processor scraps.

It took Li Yao a long time to figure out what was going on.

Maintaining the consciousness in the 'pervasive' state was a soul-consuming task. The further away one's consciousness was from the center, the more complicated the intrusion structure would be, and the more information and data would be stored in the magical equipment, which would consume more soul power.

If he merely invaded a crystal camera thirty to fifty meters away, he would be able to maintain the state of consciousness for a relatively long time, say, ten to twenty seconds.

But if it was the mainframe crystal processor of a starship that was heavily protected by a virtual defense wall a few kilometers away, it could only hack into the starship for 0.1 seconds and read a small part of the work log before it ran out of soul power and collapsed into darkness. In the few days when he was half asleep and half awake, his 'body' had been moved a long distance away. In the beginning, through the crystal processors that he hacked into, he discovered that the debris of the floating fortress was still lingering between Xinle City and the uninhabited area of Black Castle, on the battlefield that was wreathed in smoke.

The scraps of the mainframe crystal processor had been collected and loaded into several heavy trucks, which were being shipped to the space port far away with rumbling noises.

At this moment, the mainframe crystal processor had completely lost its external energy supply. Li Yao's soul was like a hungry ghost that had been starved to the extreme. After scanning the outside world for a moment, he could not bear it any longer and fell asleep again.

When he opened his eyes again, he found that the debris of 'himself', or rather, the mainframe crystal processor, had been shipped to a large shuttle. Maybe it had been teleported to a foreign space zone after one or two space jumps.

He could vaguely sense the fleet of the Covenant Alliance nearby. He also saw a magnificent, magnificent star fortress in the boundless space in front of him. "Unbelievable technology. Is this the real capability of the primeval civilization?"

Chapter 2784: : The Rise Of Heresy!

Most of the people of the Covenant Alliance were unable to think independently and could only carry out the commands of their master mechanically. Li Yao had infiltrated the Covenant Alliance for exactly this purpose.

Such was the threat of the 'heresy' that grew inside the Covenant Alliance.

In order to prevent the mental network of the 'main brain' and the main brain from being compromised, the Heresy Interrogation Bureau became one of the most important traps of the Covenant Alliance. Li Yao was very lucky. The wreckage of his lurking mainframe crystal processor came from the large mobile laboratory of the 'Devil's Child Project'.
