Over the past thousand years, since Wu Yingqi, the Black Star Emperor, activated the 'manufacturing factory' that had been sealed for hundreds of thousands of years, the malformed nation of the Holy Accord Alliance had proliferated at an unbelievable speed and occupied almost a hundred worlds in the remote space zone.

For ordinary people, the harsh, dry, and desolate environment was like a fish in water for the people of the Covenant Alliance.

It was because they didn't care about the environment at all, nor did they have any entertainment. Their lives were simple to the extreme, and every resource was absorbed efficiently.

But the habitable planets did not mean much to them. Most of the people of the Star Alliance lived, no, lived on starships. They were more starship-like and networked than the federation and the imperium.

The typical way of survival for the people of the Covenant Alliance was very simple. They would build synthetic farms on habitable planets on a large scale. Since they did not need to consider the taste of food and could take a lot of hormones without worries, the productivity of the farms was very high. Even the barren planets with harsh environments could support a large population.

In the meantime, the population went to the depths of the planet to excavate the mother lodes of metals and the raw materials of crystals as well as the relics from hundreds of thousands of years ago.

All the raw materials of metals and crystals were refined into new starships with the technology from the primeval relics. When the starships constituted a new fleet, they would be sent to a new Sector and repeat the process.

There was no entertainment. There was no art. There was no desire, and there was no internal strife. When there was a need, they could work for ten days and

ten nights expressionlessly and inject hormones and stimulants into their veins, which were almost dry, until they drained the last bit of their life and collapsed in the mines. They would never complain like their counterparts in the imperium.

When they were not working, they would sit in the relaxation room that was no larger than a coffin and minimize their heartbeat, breath, and pulse to reduce the consumption of energy.

Sometimes, when a resource planet was hollowed out, and the resources required to move the local workers were far less than the value that they could create in the future, the 'worker bees' would be abandoned ruthlessly. When the last bit of oxygen was running out under the man-made dome of the resource planet, they would not be scared of the death that was about to drown them.

It was with such a cruel and efficient social organization system, as well as the tireless 'worker bees' and 'soldier bees', that the Holy Accord Alliance was able to compete with the Imperium of True Human Beings, which dominated the center of the universe with abundant resources and endless experts, and even had the upper hand.

However, as a small nation against a large nation, if they could not win the war quickly, they would always be faced with the shadow of failure no matter how glamorous their initial victory was.

Lei Chenghu, the 'god of war' of the Imperium of True Human Beings, had once analyzed the two most fatal problems of the Holy Accord. The first was that their base was too small and weak, which meant that they could not be sustained for long. More importantly, as a civilization that was close to a honeycomb, they did not have enough creativity. Most of the people of the Covenant Alliance did not have the ability to act subjectively or creatively at all. They only knew how to follow the instructions of their master. How could they talk about the birth of new technology? O

They could only excavate the relics of the primeval civilization from hundreds of thousands of years ago to make up for the lack of technology.

However, there was always a limit to the excavation of relics. If the primeval relics could solve all the problems, more relics could be excavated in the Imperium of True Human Beings at the center of the sea of stars!

After a thousand years of competition, the Holy Accord Alliance today had come to the end of its development. On one hand, all the resources in the periphery of the sea of stars had been looted by them to build an invincible fleet that could bring their combat ability to the peak. On the other hand, the continuous development ability of almost all the habitable planets and resource planets had fallen to zero. They had to rest for hundreds of years before they could harvest resources again.

In other words, the members of the Covenant Alliance had placed all their chips on the table and were ready to gamble.

The strategical retreat of the members of the Covenant Alliance in the counterattack of the imperium was exactly for this purpose—a strategical final war against the imperium.

This was the judgement of 'God of War' Lei Chenghu a few years ago. It was exactly the same as the intelligence that Li Yao had received today. Li Yao could not help but click his tongue in amazement at Lei Chenghu's strategic vision.

Of course, the information that Li Yao gathered did not include the movements of the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance.. But judging from the large-scale heretical activities that broke out suddenly more than a hundred years ago, it was not hard to imagine that the leadership of the Covenant Alliance had suppressed the 'soldiers' and the 'worker bees' below them to such an extent that the machines could not stand it any longer and started resisting!

Chapter 2785: God’S Heart, Holy Enlightenment!

Theoretically speaking, a perfect civilization of bees should not have an insurrection.

But the perfection of ‘never betray’ was based on two prerequisites.

Firstly, the ‘mastermind’ of the honeycomb civilization must boast infinite computational ability. It must be able to observe, control, and calculate everything within the range of the civilization, from the direction of the entire civilization to the emergencies that every ‘worker bee’ and ‘soldier bee’

encountered, as well as the subtle waves deep inside their brain. The ‘mastermind’ must discover and deal with all of them.

Secondly, even if the mainframe possessed computational ability as vast as the ocean, it would still have to have a set of interconnected, complicated, and absolutely stable neural networks without any delay in order to transmit its will to all the nerve endings of the entire civilization in time for the

‘worker bees’ and the ‘soldier bees’ to carry out the task.

If the connection between the mainframe and the queen bee was cut off for a long time for some reason, nobody would know what would happen to the ‘worker bees’ and what they would do when they lost the guidance of the mainframe.

Unfortunately, in the boundless universe, neither of the two prerequisites were available for the main brains of the Covenant Alliance.

First of all, no matter how good their computational ability was, it could not surpass the category of the intelligent life on the carbon basis. At best, it was close to the level of the Pangu Clan in the primeval era. But even the Pangu Clan was far from having absolute control over the entire Pangu Universe,

not to mention the masters in front of them who were like monkeys in a hat.

The greatest Cultivators were human beings after all, not gods. It was beyond their limits to think about the development of an entire civilization and fight against the enormous imperium.

Secondly, the cosmic storms were treated equally and posed fatal threats to the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance, which was most dependent on the network and most affected by the cosmic storms.

It was not unusual for some remote resource planets to lose contact with the star fortresses where the five supreme masters were located for more than a year because of the cosmic storms.

If they encountered complicated anomalies or were targeted by extraterrestrial devils during their isolation, the entire planet might be corrupted, and a lot of devils and heretics would appear.

In short, it was like the Great Dao of Cultivation that the Imperium of True Human Beings believed in. If it was perfect in theory, it was indeed possible to build a prosperous nation and lead the civilization of mankind to a new peak. But in fact, such a perfect condition was simply impossible.

The so-called ‘harmonious, efficient, and absolutely loyal’ of the Holy Accord Alliance was just a high tower made of sand. It looked beautiful, but it could not withstand the impact of reality.

For this reason, the special profession of ‘Inspector Saint’ came into being.

The so-called Saint Patrollers were a special class of priests of a very high level in the Covenant Alliance. Different from the ordinary priests who were stationed in fixed temples, the Saint Patrollers boasted a powerful fleet that patrolled the remote planets on the periphery of the Covenant Alliance, especially those that

had been cut off from the mothership of the Grand Supreme Master for a long time due to the interference of cosmic storms or wars.

Their main duty was to patrol the place and check whether or not the ‘soldier bees’ and the ‘worker bees’ were still pure and whether or not they had been corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils and turned into evil monsters when they lost contact with the mainframe.

If there were, the Saint Patrollers would carry out a large-scale purge in the area. At that time, not only would the fleet under his command and the regular army nearby known as the ‘God Blessers’, as well as the purifiers and cleaners under the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, put down the tasks at hand

and follow the orders of the Saint Patrollers. Purging the devils would be their top priority.

Allin all, the Saint Patrollers could be understood as the incarnations of the five supreme masters. Most of the time, the Saint Patrollers bestowed thunder and fire upon the polluted planets in accordance with the will of the supreme masters. Needless to say, their strength and power were

unquestionable.

All this time, the Heresy Interrogation Bureau had been in charge of the pivot, and the system of patrolling had maintained the stability of the Covenant Alliance.

There was only one minor problem.

‘What if an Orb Patroller was corrupted by the extraterrestrial devils and betrayed their master, turning into a ‘heresy?

‘Chu Zhiling’ was one of them. The fallen Saint’s Inspector.

To be able to become the most trusted Saint Patroller of the Supreme Master, needless to say, her birth and experience must be 100% flawless.

However, it was not enough to save her from the mental corruption and depression when she was cruising on the most desolate, darkest, and most desperate planets at the edge of the Covenant Alliance.

At that time, the ‘Battle of Black Wind’ between the Covenant Alliance and the Star Ocean Imperium had already begun. In order to march into the heartland of the Star Ocean Imperium, the Covenant Alliance had drained the resources of several Sectors in the periphery. It was estimated that every

resource planet in those worlds would lose their value for subsequent development within the next hundred years.

Therefore, the ‘abandoned plan’ was implemented to seal the worlds temporarily.

Because of the limited capacity, a lot of carriers were supporting the frontline. Therefore, except for the few useful technicians and priests, most of the local farmers and miners had been abandoned and left to fend for themselves on the exhausted planet.

Maybe it was the misery beyond description that dealt a fatal blow to Chu Ling’s mental defense line. In short, she had ‘fallen’ or ‘awakened’. From being the most loyal minion of the Great Master, she had been reduced to the most dangerous ‘heresy’ in the past thousand years of the Covenant Alliance and the

founder of ‘God’s Heart Society’!

She did not seek refuge in the arms of the extraterrestrial devils. She was still loyal to the gods, but the ideology that she had been developing was a hundred times more evil than the bewitchment of the extraterrestrial devils.

“We are the children of the gods. The gods sleep in every one of our hearts. Our hearts are our hearts. Listen to the voice inside your heart carefully. It is the will of the gods!

“In fact, every one of us has the ability to communicate with the gods directly. However, a long time ago, the extraterrestrial devils corrupted the ability and blocked our free communication with the gods. They even pretended to be the envoys of the gods to confuse our hearts and our will, making us

forget the real gods and worship the sanctimonious extraterrestrial devils instead!

“In my way, through training and meditation, there is no need for the class of priests at all. Everybody can communicate with the gods directly and listen to their will while they bask in the brilliance of the gods!

“On the other hand, since we are all lambs of the gods and all are equal, why can the priests and the sages communicate with the gods if we cannot?

“I, Chu Zhiling, the former Holy Inspector, grasped the ability to communicate with the gods directly by accident and saw the spirits of the gods in person and listened to the voice of the real gods!

“The real god told me everything. He told me that the so-called ‘supreme master’ was just a disguise of the extraterrestrial devils. We should stop being fooled by the false messengers and start the real training and meditation. We should hear the voice of the god and see the arrangements of the god. Also,

we should fight against the so-called priests and the so-called ‘supreme master—these goddamn impostors, polluters, and blasphemers—to the end!

This was Spirit Chu’s declaration.

It was also the purpose of the Heart of God.

It was not hard to imagine how heavy a blow such a declaration and such a foreign society would be to the framework of the Holy Alliance under the control of the five greatest masters.

However, in the beginning, Chu Zhiling had covered everything up with her identity as an Orb Patroller, and she had been patrolling a remote planet in the frontier where transportation and communication were extremely inconvenient. The great sages had been too focused on the ‘Battle of Black Wind’ to

notice anything wrong.

When they discovered it, the sparks of ‘God’s Heart’ were already burning furiously and spreading on hundreds of planets and countless starships in dozens of Sectors.

There was no doubt that the God’s Heart Society had become a more formidable enemy than the Imperium of True Human Beings.

As a result, although they had secured a satisfying victory in the ‘Battle of Black Wind’, they were not able to press forward and pierce through the most prosperous and softest part of the imperium.

Instead, he summoned a lot of soldiers and computational ability to extinguish the flames of rebellion and the ‘heresy’ named Chu Zhiling!

Chu Zhiling’s ideology of ‘belief in one’s heart and self-awareness’ was very tempting, but it was not enough to compete with the ultimate wisdom that had governed the Covenant Alliance for a thousand years.

At that time, the imperium had been so corrupt and incompetent that it had failed to gather such important intelligence. As a result, it had missed the great opportunity to join hands with the God’s Heart Association and launch a pincer attack.

Of course, even if the Immortal Cultivators wanted to communicate with the God’s Heart Association, it was still unknown whether or not Spirit Chu, who claimed to be the rightful heir of a true god, would conspire with the Imperium of True Human Beings.

Allin all, under the sole leadership of Chu Ling, although the rebellion of the Heart of God was quite a big deal, it was inevitable that it would be blown to smithereens when the fleet of the Covenant Alliance was summoned back from the frontline.

Even Chu Zhiling herself had perished in the final battle.

Although her soul and body had been obliterated, it was not to be forgotten that she had once been the most trusted Saint Patroller of the Great Sage. Therefore, hundreds of samples of her hair, blood, skin, and even organs were kept in the gene bank deep inside the Covenant Alliance. The information about her

genetic provider and her blood relative was complete, too.

The gene samples became the experiment subjects of the ‘Devil’s Child Project in the beginning. The biologists and brain experts of the Holy Alliance created countless brothers, sisters, and descendants of Chu Ling, such as Zhou Xiuyun and Chu Zhiyun. With the help of the ‘devil’s children’, they analyzed

the reason why Chu Ling lost control…

Chapter 2786: Slaughter Goddess, Chu Zhixiao!

Although a hundred years of analysis and research had failed to find out the cause of the ‘uncontrollable heretics’, at the very least, the organization known as God’s Heart had perished together with Spirit Chu.

For a hundred years, it had been a key target for the leadership of the Covenant Alliance. If it showed any signs of recovery, they would immediately devote tremendous resources and the best troops to suppress and execute it.

Therefore, over the past hundred years, there had been almost a hundred small-scale heretical associations in the Covenant Alliance, but the Heart of God had never made a comeback until two to three years ago.

Starting from two to three years ago, the hundred years of peace had been broken. The gods would rise from the ashes and become even more cunning and secretive. Not only had they escaped the investigation and suppression of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau time and time again, there were even signs that dozens of different small Heresy Societies had been gathered together and turned into a riot. Maybe it was because the Covenant Alliance had lost dozens of Sectors in the counterattack of the Star Ocean Imperium. Even the civilization of the ‘quasi-beetle’ had dealt a heavy blow to the overall structure and caused internal strife, giving the Heresy Society an opportunity to take advantage of.

The leadership of the Covenant Alliance was in a hurry to finish off the revived Heart of God before the strategical war against the imperium began. But this time, they wasted two years and did not even know who the new leader of the Heart of God was.

Some people said that the new leader of the Heart of God was Chu Zhiling in the old days. She was not dead at all. She was merely ‘reborn’ a hundred years later under the tutelage of a real god.

It was also said that the new leader of the Heart of God was a descendant of Chu Ling, or even a ‘devil child’ who had run away secretly. It was because the ‘devil child project’ had made a mistake.

However, Li Yao could not find any evidence to support the two speculations in the confidential files of the ‘Devil’s Child Project’. Chu Zhiling, leader of the God’s Heart Association a hundred years ago, must’ve been killed without a trace. Before Li Yao’s sabotage, the ‘Devil’s Child Project’ had been flawless, too.

Having no other options, the ‘cleaner’ who was responsible for the investigation and the destruction of the Heart of God could only give the mysterious new leader a code name.

They called him ‘Puppet King’.

“A puppet king? How interesting.”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “To be able to lurk inside the Covenant Alliance for at least two years and plan and incite a series of riots without being discovered by the members of the Covenant Alliance and still living a happy life in the darkness, this guy must be one of the top experts of the Pangu Universe. I wonder, what kind of existence is he exactly?”

“If you are conspiring with the ‘Puppet King’ to sabotage the Covenant Alliance, I have to remind you.

“The enemy of an enemy is not necessarily a friend,” the mental devil said. “Just like the federation’s enemy is the imperium and the imperium’s enemy is the Covenant Alliance, the Covenant Alliance will never be a friend of the federation.

“If the Spirit of Chu’s Guild of Heart a hundred years ago still carried the colors of innovation and progress and was an object of communication and cooperation, then the Guild of Heart a hundred years later has already been modified beyond recognition by the ‘Puppet King’. Its methods are even more secretive, despicable, and bloody. Look at the series of cases where the ‘Puppet King’ announced that the Guild of Heart would be coming back again. For purposes that are not worth mentioning to outsiders, the guy did not hesitate to eliminate all the people on the starship, be they workers, soldiers, or priests!

“Therefore, if you think that finding the ‘Puppet King’ in such a hurry will be enough to convince him to agree with your path and that we can fight against the Great Sage together comfortably, you are too naïve.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Pictures that were too gory to look at passed by Li Yao’s soul.

They were all the damages that God’s Heart had caused in the past two years.

It was indeed as the mental devil had said. ‘Puppet King’ and ‘Chu Zhiyun’ were two ‘masters’ in completely different styles.

In order to highlight the existence of himself and the God’s Heart Association, the puppet king was never stingy with killing.

“This guy…”

Even though Li Yao forced himself to be cold-hearted and was used to the inhuman methods of the people of the Covenant Alliance, the gory and shocking scenes still triggered the unhappiness deep inside his soul.

If Master Zhi Shan’s inhumane slaughter was for the purpose of certain ‘experiments’ and ‘research’, then there was no telling why the ‘Puppet King’ created mountains of corpses and seas of blood.

Maybe, the Heart of God a hundred years ago was two completely different organizations, and the puppet king was merely pretending to be Chu Zhiling.

This was not an ally he could make.

But Li Yao still needed to find a ‘borrowing point’.

Pondering for a moment, his soul waves jumped up again.

“Arrange all the information you’ve collected about the ‘Devil Child Project’, the ‘Cleaners’, and the ‘Puppet King’ and look for the overlapping data.

Li Yao said to the mental devil, “Let’s see if there is any information that is closely related to the three of them!”

“…Good idea. Let’s give it a try.”

This time, the mental devil surprisingly did not refute Li Yao. Instead, it released its crimson soul waves to the maximum, from which countless runes and data flowed out.

Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!

Endless data condensed into three enormous swirls that were spinning at a high speed while attracting each other. When they finally overlapped, the data that was almost identical to each other jumped out and flooded into Li Yao’s soul like tides.

A waterfall of information immediately appeared in Li Yao’s vision. He had also captured what he needed after a long search.

It was a 3D image of a woman in a military uniform.

Her face was emotionless, as if she was wearing a frost mask all the time. Even her eyes were black and white. There was not the slightest hint of emotion on her face, making it impossible to tell whether she was a real person or a spiritual puppet covered in a layer of bionic skin. The short purple hair that reached her ears was perhaps the only thing that highlighted her personality. Other than that, she was emitting an air of destruction that kept strangers away.

However, what interested Li Yao was not her temperament but her appearance.

“Isn’t this Chu Zhiyun?”

The woman created by the 3D image was almost identical to Chu Zhiyun, the beloved monitor of Tangka. Of course, she was more mature and athletic, like an enlarged monitor.

Along with the three-dimensional shadows, a lot of information about her identity and all the files that the mental devil had collected about her popped up.

Chu Zhixiao, the nineteenth Devil’s Child, sub-project of the Devil’s Child Project—Slaughter Goddess Project, Major of the God Bless Army, Cleaner, Yasha Squad.

One word after another appeared in Li Yao’s vision.

As it turned out, the woman named ‘Chu Zhixiao’ was really the biological sister of the class monitor. Like Zhou Xiuyun and Chu Zhiyun, she had the same mother, who was also known as ‘Chu Zhiling’. They were all devil children.

Chu Zhixiao was decades older than Chu Zhiyun. She was the nineteenth Devil’s Child, and Chu Zhiyun was the twenty-ninth. But it did not mean that her ‘model’ was lower than Chu Zhiyun’s. The two of them were born for two entirely different research fields.

On Tangka’s class monitor, Chu Zhiyun, the researchers had hoped that she could simulate everything about the spirit of Chu from the past. It would be best if she could become the clone of the spirit of Chu from her mindset to her habits.

But on the nineteenth Devil’s Child, the researchers had combined Chu Zhiling’s seed of life with the genes of the most loyal and strongest warriors of the Covenant Alliance, hoping to create a version of Chu Zhiling whose combat ability was maximized.

As ‘Saint Patrollers’, Chu Ling’s individual combat ability was extremely high in the first place. It was hard to imagine what kind of destructive monsters he would produce if he planted a lot of battle genes and activated and refined them in all kinds of insane ways.

Li Yao did not know why the lunatics of the ‘Devil’s Child Project had bred such a monster.

After racking his brain, there were only two possibilities.

Or, the researchers would like to know what a heretic whose strength and ability to bewitch would look like in the worst-case scenario. They would also need to draft corresponding anti-control plans according to the damage caused by the nineteenth Devil Child.

Or, the researchers were trying to see whether or not the genes of the most loyal and powerful warriors of the Covenant Alliance could suppress the potential uncontrollable factors in Chu Ling’s genes.

All in all, the ‘Goddess Slayer’ project was indeed the most dangerous of all the sub-projects of the Devil’s Child project.

As a result, after the nineteenth Devil’s Child tested the peak of his combat ability, starting from the twentieth Devil’s Child, he no longer demanded combat ability and even intentionally or unintentionally weakened the combat ability of the Devil’s Child.

Maybe, a terrible accident had happened in the past. Even though the researchers were prepared for it, the damage was still tremendous.

As for most of the nineteenth devil children, they were scrapped and destroyed not long after they ‘mature’ due to overloading strength and other reasons. Some of them even withered because they could not withstand the excessive strength when they were still in the embryonic state.

To this day, the nineteenth Devil’s Child was the only survivor.

It was Chu Zhixiao, the master of the three-dimensional shadow that was revolving slowly in Li Yao’s vision, a member of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, an organization of cleaners, the captain of the ‘Yaksha Squad’, the ninth investigation squad, and the code name ‘Major’.

“Just like Chu Zhiyun, she grew up as a ‘Class Monitor’ in the Divine Light Academy. After her characteristics as a ‘Slaughter Goddess’ were gradually revealed, she was sent to the floating fortress for inhumane research. Almost all her companions died miserably during the research, or they lost control of their strength and burnt their brilliant vitality, turning into ash. She was the only survivor.

“His memories were erased time and time again, and he was subjected to crueler strengthening experiments time and time again, trying to stimulate the awakening of the primeval genes at a deeper level. In the end, he became an existence similar to the ultimate weapon.

“This time, after their memories were erased again, they conducted experiments on a large scale and were sent to the ‘God Bless Army’, the regular army of the Covenant Alliance. With the rank of ‘Major’, they were active in the battlefield of the counterattack of the imperium and caused a lot of trouble for the citizens of the imperium.

“After the counterattack of the Star Ocean Imperium, the two parties had nothing to fight. Therefore, they were summoned back from the frontline and purged of part of their memories. They were dismissed from the army and joined the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, where they became ‘cleaners’. They were also the captains of the infamous ‘Ninth Investigative Action Team’, which was responsible for investigating the cases of the Heart of God and the King of Puppets.

“Wait a minute. Isn’t Chu Zhixiao the daughter of the previous leader of the God’s Heart Association, Chu Zhiling? Isn’t he an experiment subject that could go out of control at any time? Using the daughter of the previous leader to investigate the leader of this generation? What is this? Fighting poison with poison? A new experiment?”

“Anyways… this woman’s life is truly rotten!”

Li Yao’s soul had never been so sharp before, like the crazily trembling sawteeth on a chainsword.

Was the woman named ‘Chu Zhixiao’ another form of ‘brain in a tank’?

The fate of mankind should not have been designed like that. It should not have been manipulated like a scarred puppet!.

Chapter 2787: Yaksha Squad

Li Yao held back the urge to smash everything immediately and continued watching calmly.

“What is a yaksha squad?”

He extended a tentacle from his soul and tied up all the information about the ‘Yaksha Squad’.

A thousand years after the establishment of the Covenant Alliance, with the expansion of the organization and the invasion of the extraterrestrial devils, the criminal activities of the heretics had become more and more complicated and rampant. All kinds of small-scale heretical associations had emerged.

In order to deal with the internal damage of the heretics, a ‘cleaning organization’ affiliated with the Inquisition was established.

The eight regular investigation teams of the Cleaners had been selected by the elites of the God Bless Army, the tribes, and the temple. They were all one-in-ten-thousand super experts who had all awakened unbelievable primeval genes and grasped special abilities that were unimaginable for ordinary Cultivators.

However, they were all the most devoted lambs of the gods. Their thinking was still limited to the brilliance of the gods. It was impossible for them to imagine how insane and unscrupulous the most evil and insane heretics were.

They could hunt down and extinguish ordinary small-scale heretical organizations, but when they were faced with the God’s Heart Association, which was led by the puppet king, the most secretive and evil heretic organization seemed powerless.

“If the reactions of every part of the system are the same, it will become the system’s fatal flaw. When everyone thinks in the same normal mode, it is impossible to grasp the thoughts of a lunatic and find the loopholes.

“Only a lunatic can understand a lunatic. Only a heretic can capture a heretic. Only an evil ghost whose seven emotions and six desires have expanded to the limit and broke the limits of all laws can smell the scent of danger and capture someone as evil as them.

With such thoughts in mind, the Cleaners, no, the most notorious ‘Ninth Special Investigative Team’, or ‘Yaksha Squad’, was established.

‘Yaksha’ meant ‘ghost eaters’.

The yaksha squad was the same. They were enveloped in darkness and emitting an evil aura. The other eight regular investigation teams were wary of them and avoided them.

The main members of the team were all ‘experimental subjects’ of the most dangerous projects in the Covenant Alliance. Although they had gained unparalleled capability through the brutal strengthening experiments and the high-intensity battlefield screening, their mental state was extremely unstable, too. In order to grasp the behavior patterns of the heretics and understand the criminal motives of the heretics, they had been injected with a lot of stimulant drugs that could awaken the seven emotions and six desires in their brain, which often led them to the forbidden areas that ordinary people of the Covenant Alliance had never heard of.

Maybe, the birth of the yaksha squad, the confrontation between them and the God’s Heart Association and the puppet king, was a large-scale experiment to test whether or not dangerous experiment subjects such as ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao would sink deeper and deeper in the process of capturing the puppet king and completely lose control.

Since most of the members were dangerous test subjects who were on the verge of losing control, the casualty rate of the yaksha squad was extremely high. Often, a ‘dangerous test subject’ would be sent from the research centers of various projects to the yaksha squad less than three months ago. They would either be killed in the pursuit of the heretics, or they would lose control due to the overwhelming emotions and strength and had to be ‘recovered’ or even ‘cleaned’ by their companions.

In short, very few ‘dangerous experimental subjects’ could stay in the yaksha squad for more than a year.

Except for the four core members.

They had been staying in this place since the yaksha squad was just established. After so many years, they had luckily survived and become part of the iron camp.

Chu Zhixiao, captain of the yaksha squad, code name ‘Major’. Li Yao had browsed through the files just now. He was the only survivor of the ‘Goddess Slaying Project’, a combination of the genes of Chu Zhiling, the founder of the Heart of God and the most dangerous heretic in the past.

The people of the Covenant Alliance boasted marvelous primeval technology and marvelous skills. Their combat ability could not be measured by the level of the federation or the imperium alone. In the counterattack of the imperium, Chu Zhixiao had slain dozens of Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators of the imperium easily. In one of the battles, she had slain six battle-type Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators within twenty-four hours. According to the files, she had barely met any effective resistance, and the files were still lamenting the lack of Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators from the imperium. Otherwise, they could have tested Chu Zhixiao’s combat ability in real combat.

In other words, the Holy Alliance believed that Chu Zhixiao’s maximum combat ability was enough to slay a Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator of the Star Ocean Imperium.

It meant that the ‘Slaughter Goddess’ was at least in the Divinity Transformation Stage, too.

No wonder he was the captain of the yaksha squad.

Li Yao recalled that, when he was still in the imperium, he had studied the experts of the Covenant Alliance in the counterattack.

Although the counterattack of the Star Ocean Imperium ended with the failure of the Covenant Alliance, it did not mean that the people of the Covenant Alliance had never fought a beautiful counterattack on certain battlefields.

In several of the counterattacks, many Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium had indeed been killed mysteriously. There was even panic on the battlefield.

In one of the battles, the imperial army seemed to have captured the fortress of the Covenant Alliance, but an expert of the Covenant Alliance suddenly showed up and killed an entire troop of the Star Ocean Imperium, including three Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators.

At that time, nobody in the imperium knew who the brutal expert of the Covenant Alliance was. The people of the Covenant Alliance were not as fond of showing off as the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium. Therefore, they had to give her the code name ‘Bloody Asura’ according to the features of the battlefield.

Li Yao browsed through the videos of the slaughter left by the ‘Bloody Asura’. At that time, she was not in the Giant God Soldier but in a thin crystal suit, which was very similar to Chu Zhixiao’s body shape and fighting style.

Could it be her?

No wonder!

The second member was Yuan Kou, deputy captain and code name ‘Titan’.

Hundreds of experiment files appeared in Li Yao’s vision.

A giant covered in wounds was lying on the metal operating table that had been locked up by various chains and seals.

He was at least four to five meters tall, and his shoulders were almost the same width as his height. His muscles that looked like explosives seemed to be alive and devouring his bones and internal organs, turning him into a monster made of pure muscles.

With the injection of all kinds of suspicious drugs, his enormous body was still expanding, tearing, healing, and mutating. Dense hair, glittering silver scales, and hedgehog-like thorns gradually appeared on his body, which soon peeled off and revealed ugly lumps.

He was biting a thick metal stick hard, but even the super alloys that were used to refine crystal suits could not resist the pressure of his teeth. They were chewed into pieces within half a minute and had to be replaced by a new one.

The researchers had conducted countless experiments on the muscular man. In order to suppress the overly violent strength inside his body, they had to implant all kinds of sealed metal plates on his wounds, turning him into a giant who would carry half an iron coffin forever.

The Titan Project tried to inject ‘strong triggering drugs’ into the human body, which would trigger the primeval genes inside the body and expose the most intense activity, so that the human beings would return to the primitive form that the Pangu Clan had created in the beginning, or even the form of the ‘Ni’ people.

Li Yao had come into contact with similar drugs in the Pangu Underground Palace of the Ancient Saint World. The gene-activated drugs could turn a human being into a devil more than ten meters tall after they were injected into the body of a certain giant crab, but the victim would lose their sanity.

Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, the two Divinity Transformation Stage experts of the Ancient Saint World, had been corrupted by the drug and had to destroy each other’s bodies, turning them into spectral Cultivators who only had souls.

The ‘Titan Project’ of the Covenant Alliance was quite similar to what had happened in the Pangu Underground Palace.

However, the gene-activated drugs that the people of the Covenant Alliance used were extremely unstable. Almost all the experimental subjects in the Titan Project could not withstand the overwhelming primeval force and either exploded or turned into idiots. Yuan Ke was the only survivor who joined the yaksha squad under the code name ‘Titan’ and participated in the endless field tests.

Yuan Kou’s capability could be explained in two sentences.

He could carry a super-large crystal cannon modified from a cannon and bombard the enemy with a rate of fifty rounds per second without wearing a crystal suit or riding a Giant God Soldier.

He could also tear apart the composite plate of the starship with his bare hands!

“How is that possible?”

Li Yao was about to be dumbfounded for a moment to show his respect for Yuankou the Titan. “The monster is even stronger than my wife!”

It was only reasonable that he became the vice-captain and the heavy firepower user of the yaksha squad.

The third core member was an expert in tracing, stealth, crystal processors, and the Spiritual Nexus. His code name was ‘Seven Stars’, and his name was ‘Guan Qixing’.

At first glance, it was an ordinary spectral Cultivator whose soul was stored in a dumb artificial body.

At least, that was what Li Yao read from the files.

But after studying the guy’s background, Li Yao couldn’t help but exclaim.

“He—he is not one person, but seven?”

“Combine the remaining souls of the seven crystal processors, the Spiritual Nexus, the tracing and the detective experts, and create new ‘souls’ and implant them into a prosthetic body?

“Then, what is he exactly? A new person or a combination of seven people? Is the master of the seven remaining souls dead or alive?”

If the files of the three core members were shocking enough, the identity of the fourth member was even more shocking. Li Yao was rendered speechless for a long time.

The fourth core member of the yaksha squad was an Immortal Cultivator..

Chapter 2788: Invasion, Spider Tank!

After a thousand years of war between the Covenant Alliance and the Star Ocean Imperium, billions of citizens of the Star Ocean Imperium had been captured. Most of the citizens of the Star Ocean Imperium had become members of the Covenant Alliance after being brainwashed. They even returned to the Star Ocean Imperium as spies and became the most secretive and dangerous cancer cells.

Therefore, if the fourth core member of the yaksha squad was just an Immortal Cultivator who had been deeply brainwashed, Li Yao would not have been so surprised.

What shocked him most was that the Immortal Cultivator whose code name was ‘Black Dream’ and whose name was ‘Yun Haixin’ was actually an Immortal Cultivator who had not been brainwashed and still retained all his emotions and memories.

There was no need to fake the internal files of the yaksha squad. Yun Haixin had been captured by the Covenant Alliance when the counterattack of the Star Ocean Imperium had just begun about twenty years ago. For some reason, he had not been sent to the temple for ‘purification’ and had been locked in a special prison cell. For some reason, he had chosen to cooperate with the people of the Covenant Alliance and join the yaksha squad for the most confidential operation to capture the heretics.

“What the hell?

“Why would the Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium, whose emotions, memories, and thinking were all intact, cooperate with the people of the Covenant Alliance willingly?

“Is he a coward who was coerced by the people of the Covenant Alliance? Or is he coveting the primeval techniques and ultimate arts that the people of the Covenant Alliance gave him? But first of all, although the Immortal Cultivators are brutal and brutal, few of them are so scared of death. They do have some integrity at the critical moment. Second of all, why did the people of the Covenant Alliance ask such an authentic Immortal Cultivator of the imperium, one who is wreathed in ‘demonic energy’, to join a sensitive organization such as the Inquisition, the Cleaners, and the Yaksha Squad?”

Li Yao could not figure it out at all.

However, he was more and more certain that the yaksha squad was part of a large-scale comprehensive experiment in the first place. Maybe the mission to hunt down the God’s Heart Association and the puppet king was the same as the purification missions of the ‘heretical children’ such as Tang Ka and Chu Zhiyun in Xinle City. They were all treacherous ‘testings’.

However, the more he read, the more suspicious he became.

It was not hard to tell that the leadership of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, or rather, the super experts who were hiding in the background, trusted the ‘Ninth Special Investigations Team’ of the yaksha squad very much. They gave the yaksha squad extremely high clearance to mobilize all the facilities, magical equipment, and personnel within their range. As long as they were suspicious of anybody, they were free to kill whoever they wanted to.

The officers who were fighting desperately in the frontline, the high-sounding priests in the temple, and even the purifiers and cleaners who belonged to other operations teams were all targets for the yaksha squad.

Fortunately or unfortunately, every time they went on a killing spree and captured or even killed the leaders who were ‘absolutely not heretics’, there would always be sufficient evidence to prove that their instincts were correct. The seemingly pure and sincere guys were indeed heretics who had been hiding themselves for a long time.

The yaksha squad proved the success of the ‘experiment’ and the value of their own existence with their precise and efficient achievements.

But it was also because of their background and style that they were deeply guarded and alienated by their colleagues who had the ability to think, as if they were carrying an eternal curse.

“One of them is the descendant who maximized the combat ability of the most dangerous heretics in history. One of them is a barbarian who triggered the primeval genes and led to the mutation of super ancestors. One of them is a spectral Cultivator with seven soul fragments. One of them is a traitor who degraded himself to help the people of the Covenant Alliance. Such a splendid lineup is indeed eye-catching!”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “Then, let me, ‘Vulture Li Yao’, figure out what is going on. I will lift the curse that has been deeply imprinted on your soul, restore your original appearance, and release the greatest strength that you possess as human beings!”

“You want to infiltrate the ‘Yaksha Squad’?”

The mental devil asked.

“Yes. It is the best choice.

Li Yao analyzed quickly, “Regardless of whether or not the yaksha squad can capture the puppet king, at the very least, they are extremely unstable experiment subjects. “Maybe, the God’s Heart will fight against the leadership of the Covenant Alliance, and we will be able to take advantage of the situation.

“It’s a good idea, but there’s one thing that I need to pay attention to.

All its details are very likely to be exposed to the eyes of the Great Sage all the time. If we sneak in, the risk of our identity being exposed will be greatly increased.

“… To get rich, take the risk!”

Li Yao’s soul tightened, as if he were gritting his teeth. “Without high risks, how can there be high returns? Exposing ourselves is a two-way street. If we hide in the corners of the fleet of the Covenant Alliance all the time, of course, we won’t be exposed, but we won’t have the opportunity to lock onto the Great Sage, either. What’s the point of hiding for a hundred years? The deeper we go to the center of the enemy, the higher the risk of being exposed will be. But we will be more likely to get in touch with the Great Sage, or even… the guy who is hiding behind the Great Sage and planning the entire ‘test’.

“Then beat the sh*t out of him.”

The mental devil.

“Yes. Of course!”

The mental devil’s voice was full of confidence and fighting will. “If the yaksha squad is the target of infiltration, there is another advantage. When I was analyzing the files on the source of ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao’s genes, I discovered that she had certain genetic samples and those of Tangka. The overlap is very high, indicating that they are related.

“No, no, no. It’s not that close. It can only mean that their father genes are from the same family.”

The mental devil explained, “But no matter how weak our relationship is, there is at least one possibility, which is that Chu Zhixiao carries the recessive ‘Black Spot Gene’ inside his body. Since we can sneak into Tangka’s brain, there is a good chance that we can sneak into Chu Zhixiao’s, too.

Li Yao rubbed his hands in excitement like a thief who had seen an unlocked door.

Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh!

It will help the members of the yaksha squad precisely control the stability of the brains of all the ‘soldiers’ and ‘worker bees’ in the area and give them a clearer understanding of the intangible Spiritual Nexus. In the most complicated and ever-changing environment, they will keep in touch with the Inquisition. It is an indispensable auxiliary magical equipment for the yaksha squad to carry out clean-up missions.

The two lines of information raised subtle waves in Li Yao and the mental devil’s soul.

Chapter 2789: City Of Taotie

“First of all, we need to find the design of the ordinary model of the spider tank and the structural design of its crystal processor. Then, we need to figure out its operating system and defense system. After hundreds of virtual rehearsals, we will be able to crack the loopholes in the design. Then, we will need to set up some ‘traces’ in the debris of the crystal processor, because the people of the Covenant Alliance know that we once controlled the entire floating fortress through the mainframe crystal processor. We will have to make them think that we did it with the ordinary crystal processor hacking technology, and that our soul ran away the moment the floating fortress crashed. Lastly, we will need to set up a few traps and some ‘logical bombs’ so that the enemy will have temporary data overflow, defense collapse, and even spiritual energy overload when they are cutting the debris of the crystal processor into pieces. The problems will not be fatal enough, but they will cover up the traces of our ‘dancing’. When the enemy resolves the ‘logical bombs’ that we set up in a flurry, it will be the best opportunity for us to switch to a new ‘carrier’!”
