For a moment, the souls of Li Yao and the mental devil unfolded like the wings of a butterfly.

As the soul ripples spread out, a brand-new world gradually appeared in their vision. Countless sounds, pictures, and information streams in bright gold and crimson constituted a splendid world. They seemed to be able to ‘see’ the crystal processor research center that they were in, the researchers who were going in and out of the research center and the pathways outside, the brainwaves that the researchers were sending out, and the wider and more splendid new world made of the ripples of the Spiritual Nexus outside the research center.

“The network is vast.

Li Yao smiled and thought to himself, Who can… stop me?

…

Three days ago, at the bottom of the engine compartment at the tail of ‘Light Pursuit’, the main warship of the Covenant Alliance.

Light Pursuit was a vintage warship with a history of five hundred years. If it had been placed in the federation or the imperium, it would’ve been scrapped long ago or handed over to local armed forces and transportation fleets.

However, the efficiency and reliability of the primeval technology, as well as the near-zero requirements for the living environment of the starship, made it struggle stubbornly in the cold sea of stars for the ‘glory of the gods’.

In order to push its enormous body, which was more than five thousand meters long, to sprint at a high speed and turn agilely, it was surrounded by dense industrial thrusters. Together with the astronomical energy consumption of spiritual shields and various energy weapons, the engine compartment, which had been repaired and strengthened multiple times, almost took up all the space at the rear of the vehicle. The tubes, which were several meters in diameter and dozens of meters in diameter, were tangled together in a complicated way, making it look like a nest of steel boas, cold and gloomy, and a 3D labyrinth full of rust.

Since the materials were old and in disrepair, hot steam was spurting out from the corners of the tubes, submerging the engine compartment in vague fog all year round, making it look like a quiet mountain.

After the steam was frozen, it would turn into drops of water and fall from the ‘above’ of the gravity field like a drizzle.

The drizzle and the mist, the maze that was the color of rust, and the emotionless marines and mechanics who were wearing grey raincoats and gas masks below the maze constituted the eternal ‘scenery’ in the engine compartment of Light Pursuit, no, all the antique warships of the Covenant Alliance.

‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao crouched on a horizontal tube at the highest point and watched the glittering ‘raindrops’ falling into the grey abyss below and landing on the ‘soldiers’ and the ‘work bees’ that looked like gray spots.

She liked the feeling. She liked the feeling of the light rain on her skin, which made her feel that she was truly alive. Therefore, what did it matter if it was not the real ‘light rain’?

“14,522,14,523…”

Chu Zhixiao was counting how many raindrops had fallen on him.

“Major, help me. Major, help me, help me…”

The voice was echoing inside her brain again. There was unspeakable pity and desperation. The rain and mist in front of her eyes seemed to condense into a little girl in a white dress who opened her arms helplessly.

Chu Zhixiao frowned. The hallucinations and hallucinations were gone, like water melting in the rain.

She stared at the raindrops for a long time and cursed softly—she had forgotten where she was counting.

“Major! Major!”

This time, the super micro-vibrating chip deep inside his ear canal really sounded lackluster, as if metal was rubbing against each other. “We’ve arrived at the scene. Where are you? Are you monitoring the situation? I’m going to transmit all the terrain data of the engine compartment and the information of all the crew members on board. Please prepare to receive it.”

It was ‘Seven Stars’, the seven annoying kids.

Chu Zhixiao turned around and saw that a shaking spider tank was lying on the tube next to her. Although it was known as ‘the entire terrain’, with anti-joint limbs that could turn 36o degrees and sliding wheels that could turn at an amazing speed, such an auxiliary spiritual puppet was still not suitable to climb up and down the maze as agilely as a human being. The guy, who was as round as a toilet, was constricting his six limbs, trying to hold on to the slippery tube. The indicator light on his back was blinking rapidly, indicating that he was receiving a lot of data and that he might fall off if he was not careful. It was quite hilarious.

Chu Zhixiao’s lips curled coldly.

“Let’s go, Red Pig. Enough rest.”

The woman whose code name was ‘Major’ stood up from the top of the ventilation tube that was almost a hundred meters high. She narrowed her eyes and opened her arms, enjoying the irregular air currents above the engine compartment. Then, she jumped down softly, not forgetting to hook ‘Red Pig’, her spider tank, with her toes and fall into the grey mist together with her.

The spider tank was clearly a cold machine, but it was displaying an ’emotion’ that was even more vivid than that of the citizens of the Covenant Alliance. Its six limbs were shivering hard, as if it were trying to grab something nearby. The crystal cameras embedded in the nine rails on its head were spinning crazily, too, as if they were scared out of their wits. It was truly hilarious.

The moment they were about to land on the ground, Chu Zhixiao seemed to have stepped on something solid in midair. He spun weirdly and picked up a spider tank. The man and the tank, which had a combined weight of more than half a ton, fell to the ground like dry leaves without making any sound.

The six limbs of the spider tank were still shivering. There was no telling whether it was because the sudden weightlessness from the fall affected its balance system or because of the lingering fear.

Chu Zhixiao, on the other hand, immediately assumed the role of the captain of the yaksha squad. His face was covered in grey frost as he observed everything around him coldly.

From a height of almost a hundred meters, the marines and mechanics gathered in the engine compartment were no different than usual.

When he was among them, Chu Zhixiao immediately sensed the fear and the intense scent of blood.

The facial features of almost all the marines and mechanics were twisted to the extreme. Panic and fear were written all over their faces, which were extremely abnormal for the citizens of the Covenant Alliance.

The surroundings had been sealed by the soldiers of the God Bless Army, the purifiers, and the cleaners. Chu Zhiling saw that the cleaners had deployed the second and the fourth investigation teams. Now that the two teams had shown up at the same time, together with the ‘Yaksha Squad’, it was a serious crime.

That was the result of the emotional scan, too.

Chu Zhixiao did not need to raise his wrist at all. Through the intense vibration of his crystal processor, he knew that the emotional waves gathered in the narrow space were beyond the threshold and were enough to trigger mass hysteria. All the peaceful, pure, and loyal marines and mechanics were infected by the virus named ’emotion’.

Although they could only sense ‘fear’ right now, it was like the collapse of a dam. If they were left alone, other emotions would soon follow and swallow them.

“Everybody in the engine compartment, whether they are exposed to the heretics or not, will have to undergo ‘deep purification’ on a large scale.

Deep in thought, Chu Zhiyun glanced at the purifiers in the anti-vaccination suits. “That’s why so many purifiers have gathered here.

“However, if we don’t find the source of the infection, it will be useless no matter how many times we purify it.

“The smell of blood is getting stronger and stronger. There is also a hint of sweetness mixed in. But for the ordinary people who are used to synthesized food, it is indeed an irresistible sweetness. This is… the smell of ‘Taotie’. Is it you, puppet king?”.

Chapter 2790: Ghoul

In the cordon with flickering lights, two teams of purifiers and cleaners were confronting each other.

Although they were both subordinates of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau, one of them was responsible for the purification of the devils, while the other was responsible for the cleaning of the devils. However, there were always devils when there were devils. On the other hand, wherever there were devils, there were bound to be a lot of objects that were contaminated by the demonic energy. Therefore, the purifiers and the cleaners often had overlapping jobs. For the privilege and the right to command, there would be conflicts, too. They were not ‘worker bees’ without self-awareness. In the long-term contact with the devils and the devils, they would more or less be affected and produce thoughts and emotions. At the very least, for the sake of work, they had to simulate similar things with their computational ability.

However, when Chu Zhixiao showed up, the dispute between the cleaners and the purifiers came to an abrupt end. They stared at her in a weird way at the same time, as if she were some kind of alien that had been contaminated by viruses.

Chu Zhixiao was already used to such gazes.

She pulled the cordon and walked straight to the place where the smell of blood was the strongest.

“Major Chu—”

One of the cleaners stopped them stiffly. “It’s just a regular ‘Ghoul incident’. Four to five teams of cleaners and cleaners are already here. I don’t think we need to bother you… The yaksha squad should be enough, right?”

“The regular ‘Ghoul Incident’?”

Chu Zhixiao did not even bother to look at the cleaner. He said coldly, “Routine incidents of ghouls cannot be carried out so fast. In only twenty-four hours, almost a thousand ghouls have been born. Even the priests of the Kindest Clan are involved. This is not a situation that you can deal with. Go away.”

She tried to push away the purifiers in front of her.

But before her hand touched him, he jumped away as if he had been electrocuted.

There was barely any emotion in Chu Zhixiao’s eyes. He strode forward emotionlessly.

The cleaners and cleaners in front of her seemed to have been separated by rocks and automatically cleared a path for her to the first scene where blood was flowing everywhere.

This was a mixed room for the accelerant. In the narrow, half-open cabin, almost ten dead bodies were lying on the ground. There were obvious traces of animal bites on the throats, arms, and even the chests of all the dead bodies. The most miserable one had even lost all his internal organs. His ribs pierced into the sky like broken spears.

The bloody and disgusting scene did not affect Chu Zhixiao at all. She glanced over all the broken bodies indifferently, before she stomped on the ground in the pool of blood with her thick-soled boots and walked toward the cabin at the back.

A ferocious beast seemed to be locked in the cabin at the back. Brutal roars were echoing nonstop. Crash! Crash! Crash! The cabin walls that were several inches thick were bulging because of the collisions.

“What’s the situation? Are they ‘ghouls’?”

Before Chu Zhixiao stepped into the second cabin, he suddenly asked.

“Yes. They are indeed pitiful creatures that have been infected by the ‘Tao Tie Virus’ and turned into ghouls. Also, judging from the enhancement of their strength and their aggressiveness, they are some kind of upgraded version of the virus. Their infectivity, lethality, and threat have all been significantly improved.

An unusually mellow voice came from the second cabin.

If a person’s appearance was determined by their voice, then the owner of the voice must be a peerlessly handsome man.

However, judging from the uncanny expressions of the cleaners and the purifiers nearby, who seemed to be confronting a great enemy, there should not have been anybody in the second cabin ten seconds ago.

When Chu Zhixiao walked into the second cabin, the first thing he saw was a… weirdo sealed in a corner by chains as thick as arms.

It—he was still wearing the ragged uniform of a warship soldier, but the skin below the uniform had turned a deep, burning red. Thick veins were bulging from his limbs to his abdomen, making his abdomen deformed and swollen, making him look like a pregnant woman.

There was no longer any sincerity or obedience in his dirty eyes. Instead, he seemed to have regressed into a hungry animal whose brain had been occupied by primitive desires.

Judging from his drooling appearance, he seemed to be very interested in the “meat fragrance” emitted by Chu Zhixiao. He wanted to pounce on Chu Zhixiao and bite him hard, but he was tied up by chains and could not get away no matter how hard he struggled. He was so anxious that he roared crazily and crashed into the steel plates on the wall of the cabin. His head was bleeding, and he was a mess.

A hair-raising scene happened!

He was attracted by the smell of his own blood after he crashed into the wall of the cabin. Sticking out his tongue, he licked his blood greedily until every drop of blood was gone. But he was not satisfied yet. He shook his head again and hit the wall heavily, leaving a lot of blood behind.

It seemed that, if nobody stopped him, he could continue crashing and licking until he was killed or devoured!

Any normal person would have screamed when they saw such a weird scene.

However, other than the monster, there were two other people in the cabin, including ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao and another middle-aged man whose face was as fair as jade, whose eyes were as bright as stars, and whose voice was even more ‘handsome’. They were both far away from the ‘standards of a normal person’ of the three forces of the federation, the Covenant Alliance, and the imperium.

Chu Zhixiao lost interest after only one glance at the ‘ghoul’. He turned around and looked at the handsome, middle-aged man whose long hair was fluttering in the wind. The man was ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin, one of the four core members of the yaksha squad who had joined the Covenant Alliance with a clear head and a complete memory.

“I just snuck into the guy’s brain and saw what happened in the engine compartment over the past twenty-four hours. It was indeed a typical scene of the outbreak of the ‘Taotie Virus’, except that the outbreak was faster and more brutal than the previous ones.

A cluster of vague black fog lingered in Yun Haixin’s eyes, hiding his real eyes and emotions in a black dream. He said, “Besides, one of the three priests on Light Pursuit has indeed mutated into a ‘ghoul’. The Taotie Virus in the past was not as deadly as this one. Even the most ‘sacred’ and ‘solidest’ priests, as well as the most virtuous people, have become its slaves. Haha!”

Chu Zhixiao ignored the undisguised mockery in Yun Haixin’s words and fell into deep thought.

The ‘Taotie Virus’ that had been spreading on every planet and every fleet of the Covenant Alliance over the past two years was not a real virus but a ‘mental drug’ that affected the human brain directly.

Compared to the ‘devilized people’ of the Imperium of True Human Beings, the people of the Covenant Alliance boasted amazing telepathic thoughts and brainwave resonation. They would open their brain at certain times every morning and night and bask in the holy light. A lot of simple commands were transmitted directly through the brainwaves.

Such a way of communication and social structure meant that their brains were more fragile and prone to the intrusion and interference of ill-intentioned people. As long as the brain of one member of the Covenant Alliance was invaded and everybody opened their brain during the Holy Light Ritual, it was very likely that they would be infected and turn into a large-scale outbreak of emotions and viruses.

The ‘Taotie Virus’ was a drug that acted directly on the brain and stimulated the primitive desires deep inside the soul. More precisely speaking, it stimulated the appetite of human beings.

When such a secret and fatal drug was implanted into the brain through the Spiritual Nexus, telepathic thoughts, or hypnosis, it would trigger the appetite that had been suppressed for a long time.

In the beginning, the ‘Taotie Virus’ would deceive one’s taste buds and nerves so that one would be able to taste the delicious food that tasted like chewing wax.

It was not hard to imagine how soul-stirring the sudden explosion of taste buds was for the people of the Covenant Alliance who had been drinking tasteless paste their entire life.

The poor people of the Covenant Alliance were often captured by the ‘Taotie Virus’ in the blink of an eye and turned into slaves of their appetite. But just like all drugs, the mental drugs had an ever-increasing ‘unfulfilled’ problem. The more they ate, the deeper they sank into the food, the more unsatisfied they became. The more unsatisfied they were, the more they had to swallow more and more food. In the end, the stimulation of the mental drugs gradually faded away. No matter how much synthesized food they ate, they could not taste any sweetness from it. At that time, the people whose mouths had been spoiled by the ‘Taotie Virus’ could not stand the dullness of synthesized food any longer.

Right now, their belief in the gods and their reverence for their master had long been destroyed and devoured by the glutton in their stomachs. They no longer cared about any rules or teachings. They would do anything to find fresher and sweeter food—real food, not sticky excrement!

Unfortunately, on most planets and starships where resources were scarce, especially in the range that the bottom-level ‘worker bees’ and ‘soldier bees’ could reach, there was only one thing that could be called ‘delicious food’.

They were each other’s flesh and blood.

Just like that, ‘ghouls’ were born.

His brain had been deeply corroded and paralyzed by the ‘Taotie Virus’. His consciousness had been controlled by his intense appetite. He would bite anyone he saw and eat meat whenever he saw them. He would suck their blood, chew their bones, and devour their internal organs. He would enjoy incomparable pleasure while he was swallowing their blood—this was the typical image of a ghoul, the sad victim of the Taotie Virus.

There was no doubt that the ‘Taotie Virus’ was an evil weapon that the ‘Puppet King’, the new leader of the Heart of God, created to overturn the Covenant Alliance.

Moreover, over the past year, the puppet king had been modifying and upgrading the damage of the ‘Taotie Virus’.

In the beginning, it was merely appetizing and bloodthirsty, but later, it was enough to trigger the secretion of adrenaline in one’s body, burning away decades of vitality in the blink of an eye and turning one into a monster with immense strength, amazing speed, and sharp claws and teeth. The shocking ‘Ghoul Outbreaks’ were all masterpieces of the puppet king..

Chapter 2791: Bloody Labyrinth

Before, the infection of the ‘Taotie Virus’ was limited to the ordinary ‘soldiers’ and ‘workers’.

These fellows, whose brains had been ‘purified’ many times to the point that they were completely blank, were as innocent and naïve as children. They were most likely to be attacked by various kinds of mental drugs.

However, with the multiple experiments and mutations of the ‘Puppet King’, the targets of the ‘Taotie Virus’ were gradually upgraded from ordinary people to special professionals. The cleaners, the purifiers, the lurkers, and the middle-level and low-level commanders of the God Bless Army were not spared.

But this time, the highest-level member of the Covenant Alliance who turned into a zombie turned out to be a priest.

Almost all the priests were the kindest people with pure bloodlines, firm beliefs, and pure ideologies. They were responsible for performing the ‘Holy Light Ritual’ for a large number of people of the Covenant Alliance, monitoring the thoughts of thousands of people, and guarding against the pervasive extraterrestrial devils. They were the core of a combat unit.

Take Chasing Light for example. There was only one ‘Grand Sacrifice’ and two ‘Small Sacrifice’ on such a large warship. Their status was even higher than that of the captain, the first mate, and the fire control officer. They were the real managers.

But what was happening right now was that one of the ‘minor sacrifices’ had been infected by the Taotie Virus and degenerated into a certain… strengthened, mutated version of a zombie. The fingers and face of his boss, the only ‘major sacrifice’, had been gnawed away. Then, he had spread the virus and fled to the depths of the engine compartment.

It was a rarely-seen infection of the Kindest Beings.

It was also the reason why the ‘Yaksha Squad’, a rarely-seen, cursed black team, had come to ‘Light Chaser’ in the first place.

“In my opinion, you are too stupid.

Maintaining the pride of the Immortal Cultivators and the memory of the people of the imperium, Yun Haixin’s mellow voice was filled with cold mockery all the time. “There’s a saying that ‘it’s better to let go of something than to let go of it’. It is the natural instinct of human beings to enjoy delicious food. How can you forbid it? Even if you have been eating the tasteless paste since you were born, you can only suppress your taste buds. Once your taste buds are opened, it will be like a flood that cannot be stopped.

“What do you mean by ‘best of the world’? What do you mean by ‘priest’? Hehe. When you taste the sweetness of human flesh, don’t you forget your beliefs and loyalty?

“If they had tasted all the delicious food like the Immortal Cultivators did, they wouldn’t have been so crazy about the flesh and blood of human beings, no matter how infected they were.

The commanders of the teams of the purifiers and the cleaners happened to squeeze into the narrow cabin. Yun Haixin’s outrageous declaration made them all dumbfounded, not knowing what to do.

Their dullness made Yun Haixin even more excited.

It was as if he had fallen into the depths of the Covenant Alliance as an Immortal Cultivator. The only fun he had was to ‘bully’ the puppets.

“Why are you pretending to be shocked?

Yun Haixin was smiling more and more brilliantly. Even the black mist covering her eyes was thinner for a moment, revealing a pair of clear and cynical eyes. “Isn’t that what you think in your heart? ‘The most firm believers of the Good Race, the priests who are blessed by the holy light all the time, how can they be attracted by flesh and blood and end up being pathetic ghouls? What’s wrong with our faith and our holy light?!’ Have you never thought that way? Don’t you want to taste… blood?”

The cleaners and the purifiers looked at each other in shock and panic. They could not help but step back and squeeze to the door.

“Ha. You are scared. It means that you not only have thoughts but also delicate emotions.

Yun Haixin narrowed her eyes. The mist in front of her eyes turned into two swirls. Her mellow voice was full of enchantment and evilness. “This is a paradox. Do you know what a paradox is? Your leader could not control the entire Covenant Alliance and the universe with a bunch of idiots whose brains were empty. They had to ask for the help of some… people who were a little bit smarter.

“However, as long as one is a little bit more intelligent, when faced with a complicated environment and absorbing a lot of information and data, one will always have their own thoughts and emotions, and then doubt everything around them.

“Therefore, although the Heresy Interrogation Bureau is supposed to be an organization to deal with the heretics, the idiots who join the organization are the most likely to become smart, emotional, learn to hide themselves in front of the holy light, and… suspect that the entire world has ‘fallen’ into the new heresy.

“I can feel that you are about to become heretics or even ghouls. I have a lot of experience in that. Really. I can smell the scent coming from your brain, the scent that you are trying to hide. Are you sure that you are not infected by the Taotie Virus?”

The twitching expressions on the faces of the purifiers and cleaners were too intense to be concealed.

“Over the past three years, 11.4% of the members of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau have fallen into heresy, which is far more likely than any other organization in the Covenant Alliance.

Yun Haixin was not going to let go of the poor purifiers and cleaners. The mockery on his face was even more intense, making his handsome face somewhat twisted. “Is this the ‘Heretical Investigation Bureau’ or the ‘Heretical Cultivation School’? How hilarious!”

“Hey, has it ever occurred to you that you are actually just some kind of ‘experimental subjects’? The higher-ups summoned you to the Heresy Interrogation Bureau because they wanted to see how long you would struggle before you became heretics?

The few purifiers and cleaners seemed to be on the verge of a breakdown.

“Haha. I was just kidding. 11.4% was just a random number that I made up. It’s nothing like that. It was just a harmless joke.”

Yun Haixin’s smile remained unchanged. She pointed at her brain and gestured at her throat, before she said softly, “However, your performance in the joke was not very satisfactory, which means that you will be cleared by the ‘Yaksha Squad’ soon, just like your predecessors.

“Let’s see how long you rookies can last this time!”

The purifiers and cleaners finally couldn’t bear it any longer and collapsed. They were whimpering, with confusion, suspicion, and desperation in their eyes.

It seemed that, even if Yun Haixin did not ‘clean’ them up, they would still be committing suicide.

“Enough. Stop playing.”

Chu Zhixiao watched the ‘rookies’ of the regular cleaning troops coldly. It was not until this moment that he said casually, “Seven Star has sent the detailed intelligence, the battle plan, and the map of the bottom-level reactor area to Red Pig. The infected ritualist should be hiding in the center of the bottom-level reactor area. However, he has at least two hundred ghouls with him. Be careful. That is the real power output area of the starship. Try not to make a fuss.

“When have I ever made a big fuss?”

Yun Haixin looked at her long and narrow fingers and said, “I always talk but never fight. You should tell that to the Titan.”

“Cut the crap. Let’s go!”

Chu Zhixiao glanced at the purifiers and cleaners who were still shivering in shock and said coldly, “Keep your troops on alert outside and don’t let anybody in or out. Also, block all the information and waves inside so that the Taotie Virus does not spread further. Do you understand?”

“Red Pig, Black Dream, let’s go!”

Chu Zhixiao patted the round spider tank and rushed into the engine compartment.

“Hey hey hey, don’t compare me to this silly spider tank, alright?”

Yun Haixin mumbled half-truthfully and followed Chu Zhixiao. “You think I’m your pet, too?”

The deeper they went into the engine compartment, the darker the lights were, the denser the steam spurting out of the broken tubes, and the thicker the smell of blood.

They seemed to have entered a forest of steel that was wreathed in fog. Very soon, they could not tell east from west, nor could they see any cleaners or cleaners.

Gasps and cries were echoing nonstop.

Tick. Tick. Tick.

Occasionally, water droplets would fall from the sky and splatter on their helmets and crystal suits. When he touched them with his hands, he discovered that they were sticky blood.

Chu Zhixiao suddenly stopped.

When the fog in front of him was cleared, what was lying on the ground was an incomplete corpse. Half of the body had almost been eaten up.

“Ah!”

However, when she squatted down to check, the ‘body’ suddenly opened its eyes and lunged at her with an earsplitting shriek.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Chu Zhixiao did not even blink. He raised his right hand and slashed the ghoul into dozens of equal-sized pieces, which fell to the ground.

“Ah!”

“AHHHHHHH!”

More ghouls jumped out of the mist, from behind the twisted power tubes, and from under the rusted reactors.

The ghouls that were transformed from ‘soldiers’ and ‘workers’ were not enough to cause any trouble to an ‘ultimate weapon’ of Chu Zhixiao’s level even though their appetite had been increased by dozens of times. All they did was hinder her vision and her footsteps.

Chu Zhixiao was strolling in the rain of blood, unleashing his killing instinct as if he were breathing.

But after she slayed almost a hundred ghouls, the environment around her suddenly turned weird.

The mist made of white steam turned blood red.

The bright gray tubes and the rusted red reactors turned into the internal organs of a certain bloody beast.

Her feet were buried in mountains of corpses and seas of blood. Hands that did not have skin but only muscles and bones extended out of the pool of blood and grabbed her legs desperately. Some of them even slithered forward like boas and tied up her limbs. Some of them were trying to crawl into her crystal suit through the gaps.

“Hungry, hungry!”

Chu Zhixiao heard countless screams.

Countless scarlet eyes emerged from the fog and stared straight at her. Below the eyes were pale white teeth that were emitting a cold glow.

Thousands of ghouls surrounded her from all directions.

Some of the ghouls were even hanging upside down from the sky, dragging their intestines, while they grinned hideously at her..

Chapter 2792: Motive In The Mist

Chu Zhixiao was greatly alarmed.

There were too many ghouls.

Although the main warship of the Light Pursuit was an antique, it had been modified more than thirty years ago to further improve its automation. Therefore, there were not many operators in the engine compartment.

Most of the operators had become food for the ghouls. The outside world was guarded and evacuated by the purifiers and cleaners. The few hundred ghouls that were still active inside the engine compartment were only a few hundred at best.

But there were at least a thousand ghouls in her vision.

Besides, the speed and hardness of the ghouls were far higher than those of the ordinary people of the Star Glory Federation and the low-level warriors. Some of them could even dodge her lightning-like attack.

Even her crystal suit had been ripped open by her bloody, long fingers, which crawled into her skin like maggots and leeches. They were slippery, greasy, and extremely cold.

It was impossible.

Chu Zhixiao immediately realized that it was an illusion.

What an impressive mental illusion. Had the strength of the puppet king improved again? It could invade her brain without her knowing and create such a vivid illusion.

“Black dream, black dream?”

Chu Zhixiao shouted, only to find that there was no response behind him. He assumed that the hearing system of himself or ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin had been compromised, too.

She stopped shouting and condensed her soul to the maximum. She sent a powerful brainwave to ‘Red Pig’, the spider tank next to her, through which she shot out of the engine compartment.

“Seven Star, can you hear me? It is very likely that the puppet king has hacked into the crystal suit system, optical nerves, and auditory nerves of Black Dream and me at the same time, which will be a major interference for our battle.

Chu Zhixiao was very calm. He sent out telepathic thoughts unhurriedly. “Don’t bother about me. Just pretend that you are in a hurry and let him continue the invasion. You can take the opportunity to find out the source of the invasion.

“Remember, don’t alarm them. The ‘minor sacrifice’ that turns into a ghoul is not our target. Our target is the puppet king. Even if we can’t capture him, we must at least figure out his methods. Therefore, don’t destroy everything yet. Do you understand?”

“…Understood.”

After a while, ‘Seven Star’, the crystal processor and the tracking expert in the team, who was using the spider tank as a platform, said hesitantly, “However, the Titan has gone in. I don’t know whether or not his mental defenses have been compromised by the puppet king. If he is hallucinating, I don’t think he is as patient as you are and will solve the problem in one way or another.

“What? He—”

Chu Zhixiao’s pupils constricted violently.

Then, she heard an earsplitting explosion that smashed her eardrums like a tsunami.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

In the depths of the bloody mist, flames were raging. All the real-life and illusionary ghouls had been blown into pieces and burnt to ash with twisted screams. Judging from the destructive posture, even the engine compartment was about to be blown apart.

The mountains of corpses and seas of blood in front of Chu Zhixiao’s eyes suddenly vanished. She fell out of the illusion and returned to the grey engine compartment in the real world.

But there was not the slightest joy on her face. On the contrary, her face was filled with anger that her plan had been disrupted.

Coldly, Chu Zhixiao strode forward.

There was not a single intact ghoul in front of him. There were not even many pieces of meat the size of fingernails.

The tubes were full of bumps and hollows. Even the strengthened shell of the reactor, which was almost half a meter thick, had almost a hundred shocking fist marks on it. Centered at the fist marks, the blood was spreading out in the shape of a laser, as if he would be ‘gone’ after one punch.

The mist in front of him dispersed, and his vision became clearer.

The smell of steam and blood seemed to have been absorbed by the muscular man who was at least five meters tall and covered in wounds and iron armor.

Sensing Chu Zhixiao’s passionate eyes, Yuan Kuo, the giant, turned around and opened his bloody mouth, giving his major an innocent smile.

It was true that the puppet king had broken through his defense wall and invaded his brain, hijacking his optic and auditory nerves and creating a terrifying illusion.

The way to get rid of the illusions of ‘Titan’ Yuankou was simple, too—to blow everything up.

If an illusion created a thousand ghouls, a thousand would be blown up.

If the illusion created ten thousand ghouls, ten thousand would be blown up.

It did not matter if there were a hundred thousand or a million of them. In Yuan Kou’s eyes, they were all the same.

Chu Zhixiao glared at Yuankou.

Yun Haixin appeared from the side and observed the scene that had almost been destroyed by Yuan Kou. Her lips twitched, but she did not say anything, with an ‘I knew it’ expression on her face.

“Well…”

The ‘seven-star’ tried to smooth things over through the spider tank. “Even if the Titan did not destroy everything, with the abundant anti-tracking experience of the ‘Puppet King’, I wouldn’t have been able to lock onto him easily, would I?

Chu Zhixiao sniffed and looked at Yuan Kou’s feet.

Under Yuan Kou’s specially-made iron boots was an old man in a grey robe whose chest was painted with thorns.

The old man’s face, which should’ve been kind and holy, was now covered in blood. His sharp teeth looked like two daggers, but they had been broken.

He was the priest who had turned into a zombie.

Chu Zhixiao squatted and stared at the extraordinary zombie.

“Hungry, hungry!”

The pupils of the priest melted in his blood-red eyeballs. He seemed to have lost his vision and could only distinguish his prey with his developed sense of smell. Smelling Chu Zhixiao’s scent, thick saliva flowed out of his mouth again.

“Why do you want to eat people?”

Chu Zhiyun asked casually, wondering how much intelligence the priest had left after being infected by the Taotie Virus.

Although ordinary ghouls were animals without any intelligence, they had been like this before they were infected by the Taotie Virus. There was no telling whether or not the Kindly Ones could still think after they turned into ghouls.

“Hungry, hungry!”

The priest licked his tongue, which had sharp thorns, while he was still shouting.

“Why did you eat your boss, the only ritual on the starship? Was there… some voice that asked you to do that?”

Chu Zhixiao continued asking patiently.

“The higher one’s Cultivation is, the purer one’s mind is, and the firmer one’s beliefs are, and the more delicious they are.”

The eyes of the young priest suddenly became clear. He made a very logical response and extended his nose to Chu Zhixiao. “You taste so good. You must be delicious. Let me eat you. Eat you…”

“Did you know that you were infected by the Taotie Virus and controlled by someone else?”

There was no expression on Chu Zhixiao’s face. He was not even interested in leaning back. He was still gazing at the eyes of the priest at a close distance.

“I know.”

Ripples spread out from the blood-red eyeball of the little priest, forming countless concentric circles that looked like some kind of weird pupils. He suddenly became clear-headed, so clear-headed that it made one’s hair stand on end. He looked deeply at Chu Zhixiao and said, “What about you, Major Chu? Do you know that you have been controlled by someone?”

Chu Zhixiao blinked. Suddenly, the lightning tentacles grabbed the head of the priest.

“Seven-star, track him now. The puppet king is here!”

She shouted.

However, the puppet king was fleeing faster than she was. Before her fingers pierced into the skull of Little Sacrifice, a smile that was gradually fading appeared on his face. The crimson ripples in the bottom of his eyes froze, too, before he turned into a ghoul that had been completely controlled by his appetite.

A vague laughter seemed to have escaped from Xiao Ji and drifted into the darkness.

Chu Zhixiao stood up with a gloomy face, deep in thought.

“The strength of the puppet king seems to have improved again.”

The casual mockery on ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin’s face was gone, too. She narrowed her eyes and sensed the brainwaves that were left in the darkness. She mumbled, “Not many people in the Imperium of True Human Beings can do such a long-range infiltration. Only the Meditation Healers with the highest Cultivation, with the help of magical equipment from the primeval era, or the super experts who are above the Divinity Transformation Stage…

“Damn. This ‘Puppet King’ can’t be an expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage, can he? Are you kidding me? Even the Imperium of True Human Beings does not have any expert in the Divinity Transformation Stage. Is this guy from heaven?”

“It is quite possible that he fell from the sky.”

The voice of the ‘seven-star’ came over again. “Otherwise, why would we not be able to find any intelligence about him when he rose to fame one or two years ago? Besides, he is growing very fast. He seems to be using the innocent people of the Star Glory Federation for some kind of… training. He is absorbing the strength of the soul with mental drugs such as the ‘Taotie Virus’ to strengthen himself.

“I have a vague feeling that, if we cannot capture the puppet king in the near future and he trains us for another year or so, we will never be able to capture him. No. We will be invaded and swallowed by him instead!

“I will catch him.”

Yuan Kuo said, “Crush him into dust and blow him into the vacuum.”

“What I’m more concerned about is his motive for another ‘ghoul incident’.

Chu Zhixiao was still gazing at Xiao Ji, who was drooling and struggling hard. “This incident of the ghouls is different from the previous infections and outbreaks. No. It is not the number of the ghouls, nor is it the problem that all the priests of the Omnipresent Good Clan have been infected, but… I feel that he is very close to us. He is even holding his breath in the darkness and observing us.

“It is very risky. He can hack into our brains, which means that we might be able to trace his source and hack into his brain.

“What is he trying to achieve by taking such a huge risk? Is it just to prove that the Kindest Beings and even the priests can be infected?”.

Chapter 2793: So Close

Chu Zhixiao observed the surroundings cautiously.

She never believed that the motivation and behavior of the puppet king would be so simple.

Every time, the puppet king would cover his real motivation in the overlapping, bloody labyrinth.

The only things nearby were countless dead bodies that had been smashed into pieces by ‘Titan’ Yuankou and the humming crystal reactor.

Thankfully, the crystal reactor of this model boasted an extremely solid shell and a very tight protection system. It was barely possible to damage it from the outside. Even if it was damaged, the energy transmission tubes leading to the crystal reactor would be cut off instantly, which would slow down the

high-energy reactions inside the crystal reactor and stop them. There was no way that an intense explosion would affect the entire starship.

Otherwise, the puppet king would only need to control a batch of ghouls to detonate the entire starship.

“Detonate the starship…”

Chu Zhixiao narrowed her eyes. She smelled a scheme.

He examined the dead bodies of the ghouls, but all of them had been blown to smithereens. There was no value in examining them at all.

“Seven-star, send me a 3D image of the bodies outside. I want both the ghouls and the victims. Don’t miss any detail!”

Chu Zhixiao commanded his companions outside through the spider tank.

Very soon, the indicator light on the back of the spider tank blinked rapidly. Invisible, thin lines that looked like transparent nerves were connected to her temples, and vivid, shocking pictures popped up on her retina out of nowhere.

Chu Zhixiao focused his attention on the dead bodies.

After scanning dozens of corpses, she found something wrong.

“The eyeballs and fingers of many corpses are gone.

It was only natural.

Since his entire body was riddled with holes, how could his eyeballs and fingers be spared?

Besides, for the ghouls, their eyeballs were the most delicious food, and their fingers were one of the most vulnerable parts.

Most victims of the ‘zombie incident’ would lose their eyeballs and fingers.

“Most of the pictures of the ghouls here are missing eyeballs and fingers, too. Although they might have been infected and turned into ghouls because they were bitten off, I still feel that something is not right.

Chu Zhixiao pondered for a moment and asked, “If we remove the limitations of our thinking from the ‘zombie incident’ and stop treating the case as an ordinary outbreak of the Taotie Virus, what should we think about the reason for the disappearance of the eyeballs and fingers?”

“Major, what’s the meaning of this?”

“Black Dream’ Yun Haixin said, “If it is not the ‘Ghoul Incident’, what is it?”

“Suppose that Bloody Red’s ‘ghoul incident’ was just a disguise, and the puppet king’s real target was the eyeballs and the fingers?

Gazing at the depths of the darkness, Chu Zhixiao mumbled to himself, “Seizing the eyeballs and fingers of dozens of key people at the same time will certainly attract a lot of attention, won’t it? Unless the victims’ bodies are mutilated with the disguise of the outbreak of the Taotie Virus, nobody will be

able to tell the difference between the eyeballs and the fingers. In fact, have the eyeballs and fingers been eaten or used in other ways?”

“Anywhere else?”

‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin narrowed her eyes. “Are you talking about the permissions to control a starship?”

“T’s true that many parts of an old warship such as ‘Light Chaser’ can be manually controlled with high-level permissions except for the cockpit, but the fingerprints, irises, blood, and brain scans of the permissions must be kept.

Chu Zhixiao said, “The owners of the permissions are often the priests with the highest status on the old warships. Together with the supervisor of the area to be controlled, on board Light Pursuit, it will be the high priest who has been gnawed beyond recognition, the supervisor of the engine

compartment, and perhaps the operators of the reactor. I assume that they are all dead, and their eyeballs and fingers are all gone, aren’t they?”

“Not just eyeballs and fingers.

‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin said, “Many dead bodies have been sucked dry. Therefore, we have blood samples and brain scanners. Well, simulating brainwaves is one of the Puppet King’s specialties. Besides, the brains of many important authorities are gone, too. Therefore, fingerprints, iris, blood samples,

and brain scanners are not a problem right now.

“Seven-star—”

Chu Zhixiao’s voice was as cold as if it had come from a vacuum, “Tell me, what can the puppet king do if he takes over the highest clearance of the engine compartment manually?”

“You can do whatever you want. There are no taboos. You can do whatever you want.”

After a long time, the shivering voice of the ‘seven-star’ echoed in the air.

Chu Zhixiao cursed softly.

He jumped up and dashed into the darkness like an arrow.

Before Yuan Kou and Yun Haixin caught up with her, she had already vanished into the depths of the engine compartment.

Only the Spider Tank ‘Red Pig’ was still following its master’s footsteps. Its somewhat heavy body also relied on the high-speed pulleys at the ends of its six limbs to drift towards the bottom level of the engine compartment.

Huchi! Huchi! Huchi!

The bottom level of the engine compartment was reserved for maintenance and modification. A lot of twisted tubes, abandoned reactors, and components for maintenance were left. It was like a lifeless metal graveyard.

There were no ghouls in this place, but there was no scent of human beings, either. It was dead silent, even more horrifying than the area where the ghouls had been wandering.

However, there was not the slightest hint of fear in Chu Zhixiao’s heart. He continued issuing commands in an orderly manner. “Seven Star, send me the original structural design of the bottom level of the engine compartment, too. Yes. I suspect that there are a lot of hidden areas that are not displayed

on the latest version of the map.

“Also, if the commanéd to cut off the cockpit is to be manually controlled, the operation room should be in this place. Pay attention to the surveillance and tracking at all times.

“Also, ask the ‘titan’ to stay on it. The same goes for the ‘black dream’. None of you are to come down. This place is too—”

Chu Zhixiao’s voice came to an abrupt end.

She suddenly realized that her ears were too quiet. Seven Star, the expert tracker, did not respond.

It was not normal. At such a short distance, there should not have been any barriers or barriers between her and the ‘seven-star’.

‘The only answer was that she had fallen into an illusion again. The puppet king had invaded her brain and hijacked her optic and auditory nerves.

Just as the ‘seven-star’ had said, the guy was training his soul with the outburst of evil feelings and desires. His soul had soared to an unimaginable level in only one and a half years!

However… it would be ridiculous to trap her just like that.

Chu Zhixiao’s mind was crystal clear. His soul condensed into a glamorous saber, which raised thousands of silver lines and cut the black space nearby into pieces.

Dark red brightness appeared in front of her eyes again, which was the brightness of ‘Red Pig’, the spider tank.

At the end of the light, a slender and tall man was smiling at him with his hands in his pockets.

It was a plain-looking young man whose face was as smooth as an egg because of years of poker face. But right now, there were deep wrinkles on the egg.

He was wearing a working raincoat that was often worn by the mechanics of the engine compartment. It was full of pockets, all of which were bulging with blood stains.

An indescribable sense of danger pierced through Chu Zhixiao’s brain and let her know instantly, “Puppet King?”

“Hello, Major Chu.”

The young mechanic smiled. “This seems to be the closest we’ve ever been, isn’t it?”

“I think so. Don’t move, and don’t trigger any brainwaves, either. Otherwise, I will chop you into a cluster of bloody mist and burn it to ash.

Holding the longsword on his waist, Chu Zhixiao drew close to the puppet king. “Who are you exactly? What is your purpose?”

“Hasn’t my purpose been stated in the purpose of the ‘Heart of God’? I pursue the liberation of human nature and want everyone to remember what a real human being looks like. Living as a human being for one second is better than living as a slave or a tool for a hundred years.

‘The puppet king smiled.

“are those ghouls what you call ‘real human beings’?”

Chu Zhixiao scoffed.

“Correct must be done. The people of the Star Glory Federation have sunk into deception and ignorance for too long. How can we really wake them up without such intense stimulation?

‘The puppet king blinked his eyes. “As for my name, I don’t usually tell it to other people. But if it is you, Major Chu, it will be a different matter. As long as you promise me one thing, you will never tell my name to a third person. This is a secret between you and me.”

“Secret?”

Chu Zhixiao found it even more absurd. “You believe my promise?”

“I didn’t believe it, but it was worth a try. It was worth the price to ‘wake you up’.”

‘The puppet king said, “Did you know that ‘secrets’ are a very important part of one’s life? If one has no secrets and everything is exposed to other people, one will not be a real person.

“So, start from this little secret and establish your true personality, Major Chu.”

Chu Zhixiao sniffed. “Why don’t you tell me your name first?”

“Lam Li Yao, ‘Vulture Li Yao’.”

The puppet king smiled delightedly. “I’m from a remote place on the other side of the sea of stars. Nice to meet you. But we’ll meet again soon!”

Chu Zhixiao heard that ‘Titan’ Yuan Kou and ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin were lunging at him.

The smile on the puppet king’s face was the same as the one on the sacrificial ceremony when he turned into a zombie a moment ago. It was fading and disappearing.

Her pupils constricted violently, and she lunged at the puppet king.

However, before she could pounce on the puppet king, an incomparably dazzling ball of light exploded from the depths of the darkness behind the puppet king and swept across the entire tail of the starship, devouring everything.

The only thing that Chu Zhixiao could do was raise his spiritual shield in time and adjust his crystal suit to the ‘explosive mode’ with the highest defense ability.

In the meantime, her spider tank ‘Red Pig’ lunged at its master without caring about its own safety. It waved its clumsy, hilarious legs, trying to protect its master, only to be shattered and almost melted by the intense light and heat.

It was the last thing Chu Zhixiao saw before he fell into darkness.

“Li Yao…”

Chu Zhixiao chewed on the name in the darkness.

Then, he bit them into pieces so hard that they were swallowed into his stomach without being spat out..

Chapter 2794: : A Unique Personality Pattern

Light spots.

Colorful light spots.

Colorful, colorful, and extremely brilliant spots of light were moving randomly and endlessly, dragging thin, long lines of light that interweaved into complicated clusters of brightness and unpredictable swirls of light. In the end, all the lights congregated into an ever-changing, vigorous ocean of light.

It was the new world that Li Yao sensed in the form of ‘star spirits’, or rather, ‘information lifeforms.

In the ‘body form’, the world that could be seen with the naked eye was a human being, a pathway was a pathway, a crystal processor was a crystal processor, and the invisible network was something that could not be seen at all.

However, in the form of an ‘information creature’, the body temperature, the frequency of breathing and pulse, the ripples spreading out of the brainwaves, the rise and fall of his chest, and the sound of his abdominal movement… were all combinations of information.

In the same way, a piece of seemingly cold magical equipment turned into the amalgamation of its hardness, materials, radiation, and other information. When Li Yao ‘saw it, he immediately received tremendous information about it.

The illusionary, unpredictable network, on the other hand, turned into glamorous, brilliant threads of light that were jumping agilely like the best dancers in the universe.

Li Yao had been adapting to the new world for several days and had a deep understanding of how to open it.

He could ‘see’ that the brains of human beings were blossoming like delicate flowers, that their vitality was blinking like fireworks, and that the crystal processor next to it was unfolding in a completely different yet equally marvelous way. The ‘flower stamen’ in the middle was shivering softly, as if it

were calling out to him affectionately to attract his intrusion.

From this perspective, the human brain and the crystal processor were no different.

In the meantime, in the seemingly peaceful ocean of light, dangerous spots, light spots, and lines were hidden, too. Some of them looked like venomous jellyfish and octopuses, while some others looked like wandering, savaging sharks that were searching for anomalies vigilantly.

They were the traps, baits, barriers, and guards that the ‘Fuxi System’ had deployed in the Spiritual Nexus.

Li Yao did not want to be bothered by them.

Thankfully, after he was transformed into a brand-new life form, the memory gate from Earth seemed to have opened a wider gap. All kinds of brand-new ‘weapons’ surged out from the depths of his soul, giving him a deeper understanding about the nature of life. He could change his form in the

Spiritual Nexus at his will and cover his soul with regular data, pretending that it was just an ordinary document.

The strategical final battle of the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance attacking the core world of the Star Ocean Imperium was about to begin. Countless data were being transmitted between the main warships of the Covenant Alliance and the enormous star fortresses every second. Every detail of the

strategical final battle had consumed 99% of the computational ability of the five Great Masters and the Fuxi System. At such a critical moment, they had no time to bother about Li Yao, the tiny ‘virus’.

Therefore, it only took Li Yao one day to break through all the defenses, hack the internal network of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau and the Cleaners, and gain a lot of high permissions.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The picture of several members of the yaksha squad who were seriously wounded in the explosion of ‘Light Pursuit’ lying in the med bay for repair appeared in Li Yao’s vision.

It was especially so for ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao. She was closest to the point of explosion and had suffered the most injuries. Although she had been blocked by the spider tanks and the crystal suits, she was still deeply burnt and had lost a lot of skin and muscle tissue. She was soaking in the nutrition liquids

and accelerating the division and reproduction of her cells with the nanometer super-mini biochemical tinkers so that her wounds could heal faster.

Li Yao knew that such a treatment was the most cutting-edge biochemical technology. It almost had the effect of reviving the dead. The only side effect was the pain. The feeling of cells exploding from the depths of one’s soul was not something that any top expert could resist.

But not only was Chu Zhixiao able to bear it, she did not even blink. The wounds on her face had not fully recovered yet, and she was already studying the files of the puppet king eagerly. What a crazy woman!

Li Yao frowned at the picture.

The thicker, tougher, and more stubborn the enemy’s nerves were, the more difficult it was for him to invade and persuade them.

But it was within his expectations.

He was qualified to be the captain of one of the strongest assault squads of the Covenant Alliance. There was no way that the ‘Major’ was a weak-willed, crying man.

Right now, Li Yao was only concerned about one thing.

Could Chu Zhixiao’s spider tank be repaired?

“He asked me—What are you scared of exactly?”

Yuankou took a long breath after he finished his speech. His enormous body collapsed.

Yun Haixin continued. “Like you, the puppet king sent me a message through the illusions, too. He said that I knew why you stayed in the Covenant Alliance.

Emotionlessly, Chu Zhixiao listened and concluded, “I’m afraid you’ve all heard what the puppet king said to me. I asked the priest whether or not he knew that he was being controlled, and the puppet king asked me whether or not I knew that I was being controlled, nothing more.

The four members of the yaksha squad fell into silence.

Even in such a notorious ‘heretical squad’, some taboos had never been broken.

No one had ever asked who Guan Qixing was, whether he was the amalgamation of seven remnant souls, whether he was made up of seven different people, whether one of his personalities had swallowed the other six personalities, or whether he was a spiritual puppet that had only inherited the

memories of seven people.

No one had ever asked why a ferocious beast in the form of a human being such as ‘Titan’ Yuankou would often shiver and sob in his nightmares, as if he were terrified of something.

No one had ever asked why an Immortal Cultivator with a high Cultivation such as ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin, who had a deep understanding of the brain and a clear memory and self-awareness, would ‘degrade herself and stay in the Covenant Alliance to help the villains.

Of course, it also included the fact that no one had ever asked their captain, ‘Major’ Chu Zhixiao, whether or not he knew about their past, what was going on with their damn ‘Yaksha Squad’, and whether or not they were part of an enormous experiment.

At the very least, with the wisdom of ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin, he should have thought of it and indeed did. Now and then, he would mock himself.

But he never asked Chu Zhixiao, as if she had never asked him.

But right now, the puppet king had grasped alll their secrets and dissected their twisted humanity layer by layer with sharp scalpels, toying with their fatal weaknesses.

The meeting was temporarily suspended.

The four core members of the yaksha squad were all in a trance. They had to meditate and train to stabilize their belief.

“Thave a feeling.

At the end of the meeting, ‘Black Dream’ Yun Haixin said gloomily, “It won’t be long before the four of us ‘clean up’ each other. Maybe this is the fate of the cleaners.

Li Yao and Chu Zhixiao returned to her lounge again.

Chu Zhixiao was curled up in a corner again. He lit a cigarette and hid himself in the rising smoke. Confusion and vulnerability were beaming out of his frozen eyes.

She smoked one cigarette after another in silence.

But Li Yao wanted to break the silence—not by dancing, of course.

This might be a good opportunity for him to get closer to Chu Zhixiao’s heart and let him know what was on Chu Zhixiao’s mind. The captain of the yaksha squad, the woman who carried the destiny of slaughtering fairies, did not know her past or her destiny at all.

Of course, it was not as simple as that.

There were no detectaphones or monitoring crystal cameras in the room, nor was there any need for them.

It was because a lot of data-collecting magical equipment was stored inside Li Yao’s Spider Tank, which could transmit endless sounds and pictures to the mainframe crystal processor of the Heresy Interrogation Bureau. It was even possible that it could be transmitted to a higher level and reach the ears

of Master Zhi Shan.

Therefore, Li Yao had to slightly interfere with the data collection mode of the spider tank and send out fake sounds and pictures to cover up the real conversation between him and Chu Zhixiao.

The work took the mental devil ten minutes.

Then, he would finally be able to have a mental clash with Chu Zhixiao without anybody knowing.

“Major…”

Li Yao stuck to her..
